

tamiḻar varalāṟu

iṉṟaiya nōkkil

(paṇṭu mutal iṉṟu varai)






pi. irāmanātaṉ ka.mu; ca.i.











tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
ceṉṉai - 600 017.



nūṟ kuṟippu

nūṟpeyar 			: tamiḻar varalāṟu
  	 iṉṟaiya nōkkil(paṇṭu mutal iṉṟu varai)
āciriyar 			: pi.irāmanātaṉ
patippāḷar 			: kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
mutaṟpatippu 			: 2014
tāḷ 				: 18.6 ki. veḷḷai mēplittō
aḷavu 			: 1/8 temmi
eḻuttu 			: 11 puḷḷi
pakkam 			: 14 + 290 = 304
nūl kaṭṭamaippu 		: iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vilai 			: urupā. 190 /-
paṭikaḷ 			: 1000
nūlākkam 			: pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi
  	  ti.nakar, ceṉṉai - 17.
aṭṭai vaṭivamaippu 	: va. malar
acciṭṭōr 		: srī veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭars

  tiyākarāyarnakar, ceṉṉai - 600 017.
  to.pē. 2433 9030


nūlāciriyariṉ muṉṉurai

tamiḻ, tamiḻariṉ toṉmai, muṉmai, teṉmai ākiyavaṟṟai nilaināṭṭuvataṟkāṉa maṟukkoṇāta cāṉṟukaḷ kaṭanta irupatu-muppatu āṇṭukaḷil 

māntaiṉat tōṟṟamum paravalum cārnta āyvukaḷ,
māntamarapaṇuviyal;
nāstirātik / yūrēciyāṭik maṟṟum ñāla
mutaṉmoḻi āyvukaḷ,
tolliyal akaḻāyvukaḷ

pōṉṟa pala tuṟaikaḷilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai yellām uṭkoṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻar, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu nūl itu. innūliṉ mutal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷil ivvivaraṅkaḷaik kāṇalām. āṅkāṅku tēvaiyāṉa nilappaṭaṅkaḷum vivarappaṭaṅkaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. iyal 1il “varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa?” eṉṉum iraṇṭām pakutiyil tokuttuḷḷa pēraṟiñar palariṉ karuttukaḷ innūlai maṭṭumalla, enta varalāṟṟu nūlaip paṭippavarkaḷum maṉatiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyavai ākum.

3. caṅka kālam toṭaṅki iṉṟu varai uḷḷa kālattiṉ varalāṟu paṟṟiyavai iyalkaḷ 4-10 ākum. avaṟṟilum putiya ceytikaḷaiyum karuttukaḷaiyum kāṇalām.

4. āyvāḷarkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ karuttukaḷ āṅkāṅkē avarkaḷ coṉṉavāṟē tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. - - paṭippavar naṉmai karuti. mutaṉmaiyāṉa karuttukaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlamum tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu (e.kā: tamiḻiṉ cemmoḻic ciṟappu paṟṟiya aṟiñar kūṟṟukaḷ; avai piṟamoḻiyāḷarukkum terivikka vēṇṭiyavaiyākaliṉ. eḷitil ōriṭattil kiṭṭāta āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷait tēṭāmal, ipputtakattiliruntē eṭuttut tara vakai ceytuḷḷatu nallatu tāṉē). mutaṉmaiyāṉa pārvai nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal antanta iyaliṉ iṟutiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. annūlkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷiṉ āciriyarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum naṉṟi. avarkaḷuṭaiya aṟivāṟṟalum uḻaippum iṉṟēl innūl ētu?

5. (i) ēṟkeṉavē eṉatu piṉvarum varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷaic caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu:

1998. A new account of the history and culture of the Tamils

1999. cintuveḷit toltamiḻa nākarikam

tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟu paṟṟiya āyvukkaṭṭuraikaḷai āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum pala āyvitaḻkaḷil 1969il iruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷēṉ. 1977-81il tēvanēyap pāvāṇar centamiḻccelvi itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭa “tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai nāṭṭum coṟkaḷ” eṉṉum kaṭṭurait toṭariṉ (140 pakka) āṅkila moḻipeyarppai Nostratics - the light from Tamil according to Devaneyan eṉatu (60 pakka) muṉṉuraiyuṭaṉ kaḻakam 2004il veḷiyiṭṭatu. ivvakaiyil eṉṉai ūkkuvitta kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar irā. muttukkumāracāmi avarkaḷukku naṉṟi.

(ii) tamiḻar varalāṟu cārnta piṉvarum nūlkaḷiṉ eṉatu tamiḻākkattai (iṉṟaiya nilaiyaik kāṭṭum eṉatu muṉṉuraikaḷuṭaṉ updating forward) tamiḻmaṇ patippakattār veḷiyiṭṭu eṉakku ūkkam aḷittaṉar.

1. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar 1947. The origin and spead of the Tamils (tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum 2006).

2. alekcāntar kāntiratāv 1974 The riddles of the three occans nūliṉ iraṇṭām pakuti (intu mākkaṭal) maṭṭum (tamiḻa nākarikamum cintuveḷi nākarikamum. 2006)

3. pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār 1929. History of the Tamils from the earliest times to 600 AD (tamiḻar varalāṟu - ki.pi. 600 varai. 2007) 

“toṉmaic cemmoḻi tamiḻ” eṉṉum eṉatu nūlaiyum tamiḻmaṇ patippakamē 2007-il veḷiyiṭṭatu. ippoḻutu intap puttakattaiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭa muṭintatu tamiḻmaṇ patippaka kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷ eṉṉai iṭaiviṭātu ūkkuvittu vantataṉāltāṉ. avarukku eṉṟum naṉṟiyuṭaiyēṉ.

patippurai

tamiḻariṉ varalāṟu taṉicciṟappuḷḷatu; māntar varalāṟṟoṭum moḻi varalāṟṟoṭum intiya varalāṟṟoṭum toṭarpuṭaiyatu. palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷilum (puviyiyal, māntaviyal, mānta marapaṇuviyal, moḻiyiyal, kumūkaviyal, tolliyal, tolleḻuttiyal, varalāṟu, varalāṟṟu varaiviyal, araciyal, poruḷātāram, ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu pōṉṟavai) iṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭu nallaṟiñar potuvāka ēṟṟuḷḷa karuttōṭṭaṅkaḷai – ērāḷamāṉa paltuṟaikkaruvi nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷaic cevvaṉē
uḷvāṅki -- eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa innūliṉ pāṅkai atai mēmpōkkākap pārppavarkaḷum uṇarvar. tēvaiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil ellām cāṉṟukaḷ antantat tuṟaiyiṉ iṉṟaiya aṟiñar kūṟiyavāṟē tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iyalkaḷiṉ iṟutiyil ātāra nūṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷ muḻumaiyākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. ituvarai ittakaiya piṟa nūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟāta karuttukaḷaip poṟuttavarai āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa-- paṭippōr naṉmai karutiyum, tamiḻaṟiyātāriṭam tēvaippaṭṭāl akkaruttukkaḷaic cāṟṟiṭa ēntākavum. āṅkila moḻi marapu vēṟu; tamiḻmoḻi marapu vēṟu; muṉṉatiliruntu piṉṉataṟku moḻi peyarkkum poḻutu ataṉataṉ marapu vaḻakku, moḻiyiyal taṉmai, nuṇukkaṅkaḷ, (idiom, genius and nuances) ākiyavaṟṟai uṇarntu, poruḷ māṟāmal, iyalpāṉa tamiḻil - collukkuc col eṉṟu illāmal - tiṟampaṭamoḻimāṟṟam ceyvataṟku ittakaiya irumoḻi vācakaṅkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amaivataik kāṇalām.

3. innūliṟ kaṇṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ cila paṟṟic curukkamāka ippatippuraiyil kūṟuvatu poruttamākum:-

iyal. 1 :- tamiḻar tamiḻakattiṉ (ēṉ teṉṉintiyāviṉ, intiyāviṉ) tolkuṭikaḷ; avarkaḷiṉ toṉmai teṉṉāṭṭilum intiyāvilum iṟṟaikku 50000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarc celkiṟatu eṉpatu mānta marapaṇu aṟiviyaliṉ tēṟṟamāṉa , uṟutiyāṉa iṉṟaiya muṭivu ākum. ñāla mutaṉmoḻikku mika neruṅkiyatākiya tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiki.mu. 10000kkum muṉṉarp pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ celkiṟatu. intō airōppiya moḻikkuṭumpam (vēta moḻiyum, camaṟkirutamum ikkuṭumpak kuḻantaikaḷ), urāl-altāyik moḻik kuṭumpam (cappāṉiyam, koriyam uṭpaṭa), āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻik kuṭumpam [50000 āṇṭukaḷāka āttirēliyāvil uḷḷavai; kaṭanta 10000 āṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ piṟa pakutikaḷuṭaṉ yātoru toṭarpum aṟṟavai; āyiṉum iṉṟum tamiḻōṭu viyattaku oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyavai.] airōppāvil intō airōppiya moḻikaḷ paravuvataṟku muṉṉar aṅku vaḻaṅkiya pāsku-- ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkellām tāyāka iṉṟaiya paṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyiyalaṟiñar uṉṉittuḷḷa nācutirātik /yūrēciyāṭik moḻip peruṅkuṭumpattai (Macro family) otta allatu viñciya toṉmaiyuṭaiyatu tamiḻ. ammoḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa toṭarpukaḷai vērcollāyvukaḷ, moḻiyamaippu, ilakkaṇat taṉmai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ tamiḻukkuttāṉ vantāka vēṇṭum; itu aṉṟē kālṭuvel perumakaṉārum, moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum niṟuviyatutāṉ. (hāl, hīrās, ñāṉappirakācar, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, illic cuvitic, kīriṉperku, meriṭrūlaṉ, tālkō pāski, antiraṉāv, cuvelapil, vāklāv pilācak, jāṉ peṅṭcaṉ, leviṭ, hakōlā)

iyal 2: cintu nākarikam toṭakkam: kimu.7000; uyarnilai : ki.mu.3000- 1500 annākarika moḻi, eḻuttu vaṭivam ākiyavai tamiḻiyam cārntavai eṉpatai iṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭu nallaṟiñar tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka ēṟṟuḷḷaṉar. (mārṣal, vīlar, hīrās, kāntiratāv, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇacantira jīvā, kuruvikkarampai vēlu). tamiḻiya moḻiyum nākarikamum teṉṉintiyāvil iruntu vaṭakku nōkkip paraviyapoḻutu mutaliltōṉṟiyatu cintu nākarikam.

iyalkaḷ 3, 4: iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa (ki.mu.500 - - ki.pi.300 cārnta) caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala iruntu avai (vēṇṭu meṉṟē) tokukkappaṭāmal aḻiyaviṭappaṭṭirukkalām. (kōvar, nevil, spiyars) ilaṅkaiyil kiṭaittuḷḷa ki.mu.700-ai oṭṭiya tamiḻip poṟippukaḷaiyum (muṉaivar teraṉiyākala), āticcanallūr, koṟkait (ki.mu.1000-800 cārnta) tamiḻip poṟippukaḷaiyum kaṇakkiṟ koṇṭāl - irā.mativāṇaṉ uṉṉippatupōl -- ki.mu.1500kkup piṉṉar cintu eḻuttukkaḷil iruntu, iṭaiyīṭiṉṟit teṉṉāṭṭil tamiḻi eḻutturuvam (lipi) uruvākkap paṭṭirukkalām. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ettakaiya ciṟanta cemmoḻik karuvūlaṅkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭuṟiñar pakarntuḷḷavaṟṟai iyal 4liṉ āṟām pakutiyil kāṇka. (tamiḻiṉ, tamiḻariṉ poṟkālam caṅka kālamē ākaiyāl innūlil pāti ki.pi.300 varaiyuḷḷa kālam paṟṟiyatākavē uḷḷatu poruttamē.)

iyalkaḷ 5-8: ivaṟṟil kaḷappirar kālam mutal piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar kālam muṭiya (ki.pi.300-1350) vuḷḷa kālam cārnta varalāṟucurukkamākat tarappaṭukiṟatu; veṟum
aracar paṭṭiyalkaḷ, āṭcik kālaṅkaḷ, tamiḻ aracarkaḷē oruvaraiyoruvar tākkip peṟṟa veṟṟikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai viṭak kumukāya, paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷukku mutaṉmai tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭum (ēṉ, intiyāvum) kaṭanta āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka vīḻccippātaiyilēyē irunta taṟkuk kāraṇam, 

ētilār ārat tamarpacippar pētaiperuñ
celvam uṟṟak kaṭai.

eṉṉum kuṟaḷ kūṟuvatēyām eṉṟu poruḷpaṭa nuṇmāṇuḻaipulamikka sṭāṉli vālparṭ (1991) viḷampuvatai, iyal 8iṉ iṟutiyil kāṇka; uṇmait tamiḻar uḷaṅ koḷka; uyka.

iyalkaḷ 9-10 ; piṟkālappāṇṭiyar āṭci muṭintu. āṅkilēyar āṭci toṭaṅkiyatu varaiyuḷḷa kālamākiya ki.pi. 1350 - 1750 paṟṟiyatu iyal 9; 1750 liruntu iṉṟuvarai uḷḷa kālam paṟṟiyatu iyal 10. 1991-liruntu tamiḻ nāṭṭukkum nam intiya nāṭṭukkum (ēṉaiya āciya āppirikka nāṭukaḷaip pōla) peruṅkēṭāka vantuḷḷa veḷḷaikkumpiṉiyar poruḷātārak kālaṉi ātikkam (1947kku muntaiya) araciyal kālaṉi ātikkattai viṭak koṭumaiyāṉatu; annāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷil mukkāl pakutiyiṉarāṉa ēḻaikaḷai muṟṟāka oḻippatu; eṉpatai iyal 10 viḷakkukiṟatu. tamiḻ maṟṟum ēṉaiya intiya moḻikaḷiṉ pātukāppum inta putukkālaṉi ātikkatti liruntu namnāṭu uyvatiltāṉ uḷḷatu eṉpatum teḷivākkappaṭukiṟatu.

4. innūlaip paṭikkumpoḻutu, avvappoḻutu iyal 1, iraṇṭām pakutiyil varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa ? eṉṟa pakutiyil varum pēraṟiñar kūṟṟukaḷaip pārttāl pala varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷ cārnta, ēṉ?, eppaṭi?, yārukkāka? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukkāṉa viṭaikaḷaip paṭippavarkaḷē uṇaramuṭiyum.

5. nūl iṟutiyil 20 pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paṭṭiyalil ki.mu 15000liruntu iṉṟuvarai tamiḻar varalāṟṟil naṭanta palvēṟu mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷiṉcamakālattil intiyāvilum, ulakiṉ piṟapakutikaḷilum naṭanta mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ; maṟṟum kalai, aṟiviyal cār nikaḻccikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippaṭṭiyalkaḷiliruntu oru paranta, muḻumaiyāṉa varalāṟṟu kaṇṇōṭṭam kiṭṭum.

6. innūlai uruvākkit tanta pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ āṅkilam - tamiḻ irumoḻikaḷilum vallavar. varalāṟṟāyvilum, moḻiyāyvilum āḻaṅkāl paṭṭavar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ tēvanēyariṉ vaḻinilai āyvāḷar. tamiḻppulaṅkaḷil tam paṅkaḷippai iṭaiviṭātu ceytu varupavar. āḻnta aṟivum, eḷimaiyum, paṇpum uṭaiya ivaruṭaiya nūlkaḷai kaṭanta kālaṅkaḷil tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukku vaḻaṅkiyataip pōla innūlaiyum veḷiyiṭṭu eṅkaḷaip perumaippaṭuttik koḷkiṟōm. itu oru karuvi nūl; oru āyvu nūl. ciṟappumikka ivvaralāṟṟu nūlai vāṅki em tamiḻp paṇikku ūkkam tara vēṇṭukiṟōm.

tamiḻar varalāṟu

iṉṟaiya nōkkil
(paṇṭu mutal iṉṟu varai)





iyal 1

tōṟṟuvāy

ka. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṉ taṉit taṉmai

tamiḻarkaḷ ulakil iṉṟuḷḷa maṟṟa enta moḻi, paṇpāṭṭaiyum viṭa atikat toṉmaiyāṉatum iṭaiyīṭu illātatum āṉa moḻi, paṇpāṭṭiṉ piṟaṅkaṭaikaḷ āvar. mānta iṉa nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ mikac ciṟanta iyalkaḷil oṉṟu tamiḻar marapuc celvam ākum. iṉṟuḷḷa moḻikaḷil māntaṉ toṉmoḻikku mika neruṅkiyatu tamiḻ maṭṭumē. “mutal tāymoḻi” āyvil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷukku oḷi taravallatum tamiḻē. oru ciṟu nilapparappiṉ varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟaic cariyākap purintu koḷḷa ippaṭi mānta iṉattiṉ oṭṭumotta iṉa, moḻi varalāṟukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu paṭuttip pārkka vēṇṭiya nilaiyil uḷḷa (tamiḻakam pōṉṟa) pakutikaḷ ulakil mikac cilavē; tamiḻ taṉit taṉmai vāyntatu. es.ē.ṭailar kūṟiyuḷḷatu pōl intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi – paṇpāṭu ākiyavaiyē (S.A. Tyler. “India, an Anthropological perspective (1973) “All of Indian civilization is built on an underlying base of Dravidian language and culture”) “intiya varalāṟṟu āyvu kaḷukkum tamiḻ cārnta āyvukaḷ mika mukkiyamāṉavai. 

2. tamiḻ eṉṉum colliṉ tiripē tirāviṭam (tamiḻ, tamiḷa, tramiḷa, trāmiṭa, trāviṭa). tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa aḻaippatu tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattaiyē. tol tirāviṭam (Proto-Dravidian)eṉṟu vaṇṇaṉai moḻi nūlāyvāḷar mīṭṭuruvākkam ceyvaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala piḻaiyāy muṭikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, paḻantamiḻē itaṟkuk kaṭṭaḷaik kallākak koḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatum, pāvāṇar koḷkai. tamiḻiṉ tiripukaḷē piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ. paḻan tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭa “tol tirāviṭam” eṉpatu kaṟpaṉaiyē eṉpar pāvāṇar. ākavē innūluḷ tirāviṭam, tol tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉavellām uṇmaiyil paḻantamiḻaiyē kuṟippatākak koḷka.

3. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamulian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē kālṭuvel kālam varai vaḻaṅkiya peyarai avar tāṉ “tirāviṭa” moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccait tiripu vaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil “tamiḷa”, taṇṭiṉ eḻutiya avanti cuntarikataiyil“tramiḷa’ eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvāku paramparaiyiṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum ‘tamiḷa’ tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉar kumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantira vārttikattil

“tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva” (appaṭi tirāviṭa
(=tamiḻ) maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil)

eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ-tamiḷa-tramiḷa-tramiṭa-trāviṭa eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci.parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyai acciṭṭapoḻutu apattamāka

		“āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva”

eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻaikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉa vācakattai kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō [Linguistic Survey of India:1906] pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟai pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaaiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42m tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. kuññajuṇṇirājā Annals of Oriental Researchtokuti 28 (1979) kaṭṭuraiyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla parṉel uṭaiya tavaṟāṉa vācakattait tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟi “tamiḻaiyum, teluṅkaiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār” eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar!

4. tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟṟaic cevvaṉē aṟiya māntar iṉat tōṟṟamum paravalum; moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum paṟṟi iṉṟaiya paṉṉāṭṭup paltuṟai aṟiñar ēṟṟuḷḷa ceytikaḷait terintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. (mutalil “varalāṟu” paṟṟi aṟiñar palar karuttukkaḷait tanta piṉṉar acceytikaḷaik kāṇalām.)

u. varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa?

		varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta karuttukaḷaip potuvāka aṉaivarumē terintiruppatu naṉṟu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉa cilavaṟṟaik kāṇka.

“muṟkālattil eṉṉa naṭantirukkalām eṉpataip paṟṟi pintaiya talaimuṟaiyaic cārnta māntaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉattil uruvākkik koḷḷum eṇṇamē varalāṟu ākum. eḻutupavaṉuṭaiya aṟivunilai, maṉanilai, avaṉuṭaiya camutāyap pārvai, avaṉukkuk kiṭṭum ātāraṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkēṟpavē oru kālattaippaṟṟi allatu oru poruḷaippaṟṟi oruvaṉ varalāṟu eḻutukiṟāṉ. eṉavē ovvoru kālam, poruḷ paṟṟi palvēṟu varalāṟukaḷ irukkak kūṭiyatē.”

		“varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ paṭṭaṟivu, paṟpala viṣayaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya avaṉuṭaiya kaṇṇōṭṭam ākiyavaṟṟāl uruvāṉa avaṉuṭaiya maṉamtāṉ avaṉ eppaṭi varalāṟṟai eḻutu kiṟāṉ eṉpatai nirṇayikkiṟatu; viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ muyaṟkomputāṉ. eṉavē enta vara lāṟṟu nūlum muḻumaiyāṉa appaṭṭamāṉa uṇmaiyaik kār uḷḷaḷavum kaṭal nīr uḷḷaḷavum nirṇayittu viṭṭatāka eṇṇiviṭak kūṭātu.”
- kāri pekmāṉ.

“varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ allatu avaṉaip payaṉpaṭuttu pavarkaḷ uḷḷattil veḷippaṭaiyākavō (allatu avar kaḷukkē teriyāmal āḻmaṉattilō) uḷḷa kuṟikkōḷ kaḷuṭaṉtāṉ varalāṟu eḻutappaṭukiṟatu. akkuṟikkōḷkaḷ piṟa iṉaṅkaḷai, kuḻukkaḷaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttutalum vacappaṭuttutalum; camutāyattiṟku ilakkukaḷaik kāṭṭi ūkku vit ta l ; kuḻuk kaḷ, va r k kaṅ kaḷuk ku uṇar c ci yūṭṭu tal , atikārattai ēṟkeṉavē kaiyiṟ koṇṭuḷḷavarkaḷukku valuvūṭṭutal; atikāramillātavarkaḷiṭaiyē ippoḻutuḷḷa nilaimaiyē cari eṉṉum pontikai maṉanilaiyai ēṟpaṭuttutal pōṉṟaṉavām.”
- jē.ec. piḷamp

		mānta iṉam ceyta koṭumaikaḷ, ataṉ mūṭattaṉaṅkaḷ, ataṟku ēṟpaṭṭa tīyūḻ nikaḻccikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ pativē varalāṟu.
- car eṭvarṭ kippaṉ

		(eppoḻutum tīmaiyē ōṅkiyiruppatāṉa) inta ulakil naṭappavai niyāyamāṉavai eṉa enta mūṭaṉum nampamāṭṭāṉ.
- ṭākṭar jāṉcaṉ

		“varalāṟṟil perumpakuti uṉṉippu vēlai; mīti viruppu veṟuppiṉpaṭiyāṉa kūṟṟu”
- vil & ēriyal ṭurāṉṭ

		“āḷupavarkaḷ, aracukaḷ cārpāka eḻutappaṭum varalāṟukaḷai nampuvatu kolaikāraṉ colvatai appaṭiyē nampuvatu pōla.”
- caimaṉ vail

		“cevivaḻic ceyti poyyāka irukkum aḷavukku, eḻutiyavaiyum kalveṭṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟil cetukkiyavaiyum puḷukāka irukkalām”

“pācil paṭivaṅkaḷ uyiriṉa varalāṟukaḷait terivik kiṉṟaṉa. atupōl oru iṉattiṉ moḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷum avviṉattiṉ varalāṟṟai (kuṟippāka ammoḻi pēcunar paṇṭaik kālattil ententap pakutikaḷil avvappoḻutu vacittu vantaṉar eṉpatai) terivikkavallatu.
- jāṉ pirēcar 1879

“varuṅkālattil eṉṉa naṭakkum eṉak kūṟat tēvaiyāṉa aṟivai viṭa, paṇṭu eṉṉa naṭantirukkum eṉa uṉṉikkat tēvaiyāṉa aṟivu mika nuṭpamāṉatu”

aṉatōl pirāṉcu.

“teruvil kampattiṉ ucciyil viḷakku irukkiṟatu - aṉaivarum oḷi peṟuvataṟkāka. āyiṉum kuṭikāraṉ ceyvatu eṉṉa? kampattaip piṭittuk koṇṭu, tāṉ taraiyil viḻuntu puraḷāmal pārttuk koḷkiṟāṉ. varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu kuṭikāraṉ ceyal pōl irukkak kūṭātu.”

“varalāṟu eḻutuvataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ mikak kuṟaivāka irukkum poḻutu nām maṉatiṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa? varalāṟṟu nikaḻcciyai viḷakkappōvatu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa caṭṭaviti oṉṟiṉ kīḻt tīrppu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu alla. varalāṟṟu nikaḻcci etuvāyiṉum appaṭṭamāka itutāṉ naṭantatu eṉṟu niṟuviṭa yārālum iyalātu. eṉavē kiṭaitta cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ, niyāyamāṉa uṉṉippukkum iṭamaḷittut tāṉ varalāṟṟu nikaḻcciyaip paṟṟi eḻuta iyalum.
-W.W. haiṭu

		“varalāṟṟu nikaḻvu oṉṟu kuṟittu iṉṟuvarai neṭunāḷāka oru potuvāṉa muṭivu kōlōcci varalām. eṉiṉum ammuṭivil uḷḷa tavaṟukaḷai niṟuva ātāram irukkumāṉāl avvātāraṅkaḷai valiyuṟutta añcak kūṭātu, paḻaiya muṭivai māṟṟi (pontikai tarakkūṭiya) putumuṭivai uruvākka uṭaṉaṭiyāka iyalātu eṉṟāl kūṭa. 

		“uṇmaiyaic coṉṉāl pakaiyum kēṭum varalām eṉṟālum uṇmaiyai maṟaikkātiruppatē nallatu”
-ceyiṉṭ jerōm
		
		“coṉṉāl virōtamatu ākiṉum colluvaṉ”
-nammāḻvār

“camutāyattil nikaḻntuḷḷa māṟṟaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya uṇmaikaḷai veḷikkoṇarntu iṉṟuḷḷa karutu kōḷkaḷil putiya oḷi kāṭṭa viḻaiyum varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ taṉ ceyalmuṟaiyai māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉkiṟēṉ nāṉ. ērāḷamāṉa
taravukaḷiṭaiyē neṭunāḷ uḻaṉṟu koṇṭiruntu piṉṉar avaṟṟiliruntu oru karutukōḷai uruvākka muyalvatai viṭa oru eṇṇattukku ātāramuḷḷatu eṉa taṉatu nuṇṇaṟi vukkut tōṉṟiṉāl avveṇṇattai (tāṉ eṇṇiya vāṟō allatu āyvil uruvākkum cemmaippāṭu kaḷuṭaṉō) niṟuviṭat taravukaḷ uḷḷaṉavā eṉṟu tēṭuvatē payaṉtarum naṭaimuṟaiyākum.
-pernāṭ es kāṉ

		(“A moment of insight is worth a month of dead pull”)

oru kāla kaṭṭattil nikaḻnta varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷai viḷakkavalla aṭippaṭaik kōṭpāṭṭai āḻamākac cintittu uruvākkāmal, koṉṉē annikaḻccikaḷai maṭṭum kūṟuvatu ticaikāṭṭum karuviyiṉṟik kappaṟpayaṇam mēṟkoḷvatākum. kiṭaikkum taravukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil taṉatu eṇṇaṅkaḷai uracippārkkāmal, taṉ viruppampōla kōṭpāṭukaḷai uruvākkuvatō maṇal vīṭu kaṭṭuvatu pōṉṟatēyākum.
- nopuru karāṣimā (2001)

 “iṉṟuḷḷa aracāṅkaṅkaḷ evaiyum potumakkaḷ nalaṉ kuṟittavai alla. potu makkaḷ peyaraic collik koṇṭu avarkaḷ nalaṉukkāka ceyvatākap puḷukik
koṇṭē taṅkaḷ nalaṉaiyum mēmpāṭṭaiyum nilaip paṭuttik koḷḷa paṇakkārarkaḷ ceyalpaṭuttum catit tiṭṭamē ‘aracāṅkam’ ākum.”
- car tāmas mūr

“ituvarai ulakil irunta enta aracai eṭuttuk koṇṭālum atil nāṭṭāṇmai vakittavarkaḷ kāṟṟuḷḷa pōtē tūṟṟik koḷpavarkaḷum, mūṭarkaḷumē;
iṉi varuṅkālattilum eṉṟaikkum itē nilai tāṉ nīṭikkum.”
- jāṉ ṭiraiṭaṉ

“māntariṉattai aṭimaippaṭuttum palvēṟu ecamāṉar kaḷuṭaiya akkiramamāṉa kōṭpāṭākiya ‘ellām tamakkē; piṟar ekkēṭu keṭṭāl eṉṉa’ eṉpataiyē paṇakkārarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
-ātam smit

“maṉitaṉ oru kōṇalmāṇal kompu; ataik koṇṭu nērāṉatu etaiyum ceyya iyalātu.”
-immāṉuvēl kāṉṭ

‘oru kuṭumpattai naṭattum poṟuppait toṭṭil kuḻantaikaḷiṭam oppaṭaippatu pōṉṟatē, “vayatu vantōr aṉaivarukkum vākkurimai” aṭippaṭaiyil amaiyum jaṉa nāyakam ākum.
- āṭṭō vāṉ pismārk

“(cuyanalattukkāka) taṅkaḷāl iyaṉṟa aḷavukkup (piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum) kēṭu ceyya muyalvatum; taṅkaḷaik kāttuk koḷḷac ceytāka vēṇṭiya akkiramaṅkaḷaic ceytu muṭippatum mānta iṉattiṉ iyaṟkai”
-perṭrāṇṭ racal

“nākarikam eṉṟa col kolaikārac col - “cutantiram” “jaṉanāyakam” mutaliya coṟkaḷaip pōla. nakaraṅkaḷil nilaiyāka vāḻum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaiyaik kuṟippataṟku maṭṭumē atai payaṉpaṭuttukiṟēṉē yoḻiya atu uyarntatu, ciṟantatu eṉṟa poruḷil alla; uṇmaiyil accollukku nāṉ cuṭṭum poruḷ maṭṭumē cari. atu ‘uyarnta / ciṟanta’ vāḻkkai neṟi eṉṟu potuvākap palarum eṇṇuvatu muḻumūṭattaṉam. “nākarika” makkaḷ ceyaṟpāṭu kaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ cila: paṇṭaik kālattil rōm pēraracu āṇṭu tōṟum pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa aṭimai kaḷaiyum piṟanāṭṭuk kaitikaḷaiyum carkkas araṅkil vilaṅkukaḷōṭu caṇṭaiyiṭṭuc cākaṭittatu; oruvarukkoruvar koṭūramāka caṇṭaiyiṭṭu maṭiyavaittatu. iṭaikkālattil mekcikō nāṭṭu civappintiya astek maṉṉarkaḷ āṭciyil taṅkaḷ rattaveṟipiṭitta kaṭavuḷ Huitzalopochtli-kku āṇṭutōṟum ēṟattāḻa 50000 aṇṭai nāṭṭukkaitikaḷaik koṉṟaṉar - uyirōṭu irukkum poḻutē neñcāṅkulaikaḷaik kattiyāl kutti eṭuttu narapali koṭuttaṉar. speyiṉ nāṭṭil kattōlikka kiṟittava “iṅkuyicicaṉ Inquisition koṭumai taṭaimuṟaippaṭuttap paṭṭa 1480-1800 il āṇṭutōṟum ammatakkōṭpāṭukaḷai mīṟiyatākak karutappaṭṭa cila ilaṭcam pēr cittiravataik kāḷāyiṉar; uyirōṭu koḷuttappaṭṭaṉar. cerumāṉiya iṭlar 1939-45 kāla aḷavil 60 laṭcam yūtarkaḷai akkiramamākak koṭumaippaṭuttik kolaiceytāṉ, pala vaḻikaḷil - - nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉavarkaḷai aṟaiyil aṭaittu viṣavāyu celuttik koṉṟatu uṭpaṭa. “nākarika” makkaḷ ceyta immāperum koṭumaikaḷai viṭa mōcamāṉa koṭumaikaḷai “nākarikamaṭaiyāta” makkaḷ evarum ceyta tillai. [koṭumai nikaḻccikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai maṭṭumē mūlam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. moḻi peyarppil vivaraṅkaḷum viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
- rōṉālṭu raiṭ 1992.

		“cila āṇṭukaḷukkorumuṟai vākkuc cāvaṭikkuc ceṉṟu makkaḷ ōṭṭuppōṭum pōlic caṭaṅkutāṉ jaṉa nāyakam; vaṟumaiyil uḻalum perumpāṉmaiyiṉar nilaiyai uyarttakkūṭiya koḷkaikaḷai enta nāṭṭu aracum oru poḻutum ceyalpaṭuttātu.” 

-nōvām cāmaski 1994



“muṉṉēṟṟam (vaḷarcci) eṉṟa mōcaṭi vārttai uṇmaiyil etaik kuṟikkiṟatu eṉpatu paṟṟi nākarika ulakam uṭaṉaṭiyākac cintikka vēṇṭiya āpattāṉa kaṭṭam itu. pireñcu ñāṉi ṭakvil avvārttaiyai veṟuttavar. veṟukkātavaṉ iṭlar!”
-jāṉ lūkāks 1999

muntaiya varalāṟu iṉṟaiya nilaimaikkāṉa piṉ pulattai viḷakki oḷi tarukiṟatu. uṉatu nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟaip paṟṟik koñcamāvatu teriyāmal iṉṟaiya nilaimaiyaip puriyavē muṭiyātu. piṟanāṭukaḷaip paṟṟiyum avvāṟē. tamiḻaṉ [mūlattil ṭirevalyāṉ ‘āṅkilēyaṉ’ eṉpār] eṉṟa nilaiyil uṉ iṉattiṉ varalāṟṟait terintu koḷḷāviṭṭāl pala viṣayaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya eṇṇa vōṭṭaṅkaḷ, ātāramaṟṟa cila niṉaippukaḷ (prejudices); nikaḻkāla nikaḻccikaḷai nīnōkkumpāṅku (makiḻcci, tuyaram, veṟuppu pōṉṟavai) ākiyavaṟṟaik kūṭa nī viḷaṅkik koḷḷa iyalātu.
-ji.em. ṭirevelyāṉ

(paraparappāṉa) taṟkāla varalāṟu nāyiṉ vāl pōṉṟatu. vāl nāyiṉ uṭampil iṟutiyil iruppiṉum mikap paraparappāka āṭum uṟuppu atutāṉ. āyiṉum nāyiṉ uṭampil mukkiyamāṉa uṟuppukaḷ ellām anta vāliṟku muṉṉar uḷḷavaiyē!

-ī.hec. ṭāṉs

“oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ enta aḷavukkut tamatu moḻiyaip paṟṟi aṟintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu nī col. anta nāṭu taṉatu taṉittaṉmaiyai paṟṟi enta aḷavukkuk kavalaippaṭukiṟatu? atu taṉṉaik kāppāṟṟik koḷḷumā? eṉpatai nāṉ uṭaṉē colli viṭuvēṉ.”
-jāṉ ciyārṭi 1978

“‘vaḷarcci’ eṉpatu (ellāvaṟṟaiyum) māṟṟi koṇṭē iruppatu alla; nallaṉavaṟṟaik kaiviṭāmal iruppatē vaḷarcci... varalāṟṟai maṟappavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭṭa iṉṉalkaḷai iṭaiviṭātu mīṇṭum mīṇṭum peṟuvar
-jārj cantayāṉā

“iṉṟu nikaḻvaṉavum, aṉṟu nikaḻntaṉavum iraṇṭumē varuṅkālattai niruṇayikkalām; varuṅkālam eṉpatē iṟanta pi. irāmanātaṉ 9 kālattiliruntu varuvatutāṉ”...... “uṇmai cuṭum, uṇmaiyai nām tāṅkik koḷḷa iyalātu.”
-ṭi.es. eliyaṭ

“meyyāṉa nāṇayattuṭaṉ pōli nāṇayamum puḻaṅkum poḻutu piṉṉatutāṉ viraintu kaimāṟip perumpuḻakkattil irukkum; atēpōl paṇpaṭṭa oḻukkattaip pōlik kalācāram viraṭṭiviṭum”
-villiyam kōlṭiṅ

‘varalāṟu eḻutum nām namkālattavar cārpil mānta iṉattil muntaiya naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaip paṟṟi matippīṭu ceykiṟōm. varalāṟṟāyvu ceyvatākak kūṟikkoṇṭu poḻutu pōkkupavar muntai varalāṟṟu naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaik kuṟittu itu aṟivuṭaiyatu, atu mūṭattaṉamāṉatu; itu matukaiyuṭaiyatu, atu kōḻaittaṉam; icceyal naṉṟu, acceyal tītu; eṉṟavāṟu matippiṭṭuk kūṟum poṟuppait taṭṭik kaḻittal ollātu. kāyaṭitta varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ namakkut tēvaiyillai.”
-ār.ji. kāliṅvuṭ

2. varalāṟṟu varaiviyal (Historiography)māṇavar nalaṉ karuti mēṟkaṇṭa kūṟṟukaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ kīḻē tarappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.

		History is a reconstruction of elements of the past in the mind of a human being of a later generation...... In principle there will be multiple histories of any given period, each congruent to the mental world, social purposes, and sources available to the person who creates it.

		Since the recreation of the past takes place in the mind of the individual historian which has been shaped by his personal experience and world view, the unbiased historian is an unattainable ideal.

		By the very nature of the historical discourse there can be no final truth - Gary Beckman “The limits of credulity” Journal of American Oriental Society 125.3 (2005)

		(“History”) is always a created ideology with a purpose, designed to control individuals or motivate societies, or inspire classes...... to strengthen the purpose of those who possessed power..... and reconcile those who lacked it.

- J.H. Plumb (1969) The death of the past (quoted by Beckman)

		“History is indeed little more than the register of the crimes follies and misfortunes of mankind” 
- Sir Edward Gibbon

		“Most history is guessing and the rest is prejudice.
- Will and Ariel Durant

		Official history is believing the murderers at their word.
- Simone weil

		“The pen is at least as great a liar as the tongue” 
	“Words may also be fossil history, the history of a nation’s wanderings” 
-John Fraser 1879. Etruscans; were they celts?” Edinburgh.
 
		It needs a rarer genius to restore the past than to foretell the future. 
-Anatole France

		“(Statistics should not be used) as a drunken man uses lamp posts, for support rather than information” 

		“Where the evidence is meagre, it is well to remember that what is at issue is not a point of exact law but an event of history, a field in which absolute truth is unattainable and interpretation must ordinarily resort to a certain amunt of surmise” (W.W. Hyde: Ancient Greek Mariners, N.Y. 1974)

		“It is appropriate to challenge conventional wisdom even where there may be no equally comprehensive wisdom to substitute in its place”

		“If an offence come out of the truth, better is it that the offence come than that the truth be concealed” (St Jerome)

		If the historian is to make breakthrough to new views of social change and to contribute to the development of social theory, I have argued that his mode of work must change. He has to start not with data but with ideas and then ask what data are available or could be found to test and refine his ideas.”
- Bernard S. Cohn. Oct 1970. “Society and social change under
the Raj” South Asian Review 4-1.

 		“Studying history devoid of applicable theory is to set to sea without a compass, while postulating theory, free of factual examination is to build a castle on quicksand.
- Noboru Karashima, in his preface to Karashima Omnibus
2001.

		“The modern state is not a commonwealth... (it is) nothing but a certain conspiracy of rich men procuring their own comforts under the name and title of the commonwealth” 
- Sir Thomas More

		

No government has ever been, or can ever be, wherein time - servers and block heads will not be uppermost. 
- John Dryden

		(rich men follow) “the vile maxim of the masters of mankind - all for themselves and nothing for other people” 
-Adam Smith
		“Universal suffrage is the government of a house by its nursery
-Otto Von Bismark

		“Men do as much evil as they can and as much as they must”
-Bertrand Russell

		“Civilization” like “freedom” and “democracy” is a word that kills. I use it in its literal sense, as shorthand for a settled life in towns and cities. If the word means anything at all, it means only this: The moral values commonly attached to it are nonsense. Civilized people have practised the Roman circus, the Aztec sarifice, the Spanish Inquisition, the gassing of jews. Uncivilized ones have behaved no worse.
- Ronald Wright 1992. Stolen Continents - the Indian story.
John Murray; pp 424. see p 98

		Democracy is nothing more than formal voting, while Government policies do not attend to the necessities of the most impoverished”
-Noam Chomsky.. 1994. World Order Old and New

		“the grave task before civilisation now is a necessary rethinking of the entire meaning of progress (a term that Tocqueville abhorred, but Hitler did not)
- John Lukacs. 1999



		History throws light on the present. you cannot understand your own country, still less any other, unless you know something of its history. You cannot even understand your own personal opinions. prejudices and emotional reactions unless you know what is your heritage as an Englishman” 
-G.M. Trevelyan

		Contemporary history is like the tail of a dog; it looks more conspicuousley agitated than the rest, but all the most important parts have preceded it” 
-E.H. Dance

		“Tell me how much a nation knows about its own language and I will tell you how much that nation cares about its own identity” 
John Ciardi (Chicago Tribune: 27.8.78). 

		Progress, far from consisting of change depends on retentiveness.... Those who disregard the past are bound to repeat it.
- George Santayana.

		“Time present and time past are both perhaps present in time future”
		“We cannot stand much reality” 
- T.S. Eliott

		“Just as bad money drives out good, so inferior culture drives out superior” 
- William Golding.

		“We are the present of man, passing judgement on his own corporate past. What we cannot do, is to continue playing with historical research and yet shirk the responsibility of judging the actions we narrate; saying this is wise, that foolish; this courageous, that cowardly; this well done, that ill”

		- R.G. Colingwood (1999 posthumous: Ed by W.H. Dray and another) The Principles of history and other writings in philosophy of History. Oxford; OUP.

ṅa. mānta iṉattōṟṟamum paravalum

1. puṭaviyiṉ (pirapañcattiṉ) akavai cumār 1400 kōṭi āṇṭuvarai irukkalām. ulakam uṭpaṭa ñāyiṟṟuk kuṭum pattiṉ vayatu ēṟattāḻa 450 kōṭi āṇṭu ākum. ulakil mutaṉ mutalil uyirkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu ēṟattāḻa 300 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉartāṉ. mutalil tōṉṟiyavai ‘pāk ṭīriyā’ pōṉṟa ōraṇu uyirikaḷē. iṉṟuḷḷa aṉaittuc ceṭikoṭikaḷum vilaṅku - paṟavaikaḷum orē mūlattil tōṉṟiyavaiyē ākum. ivvuyiriṉa vakaikaḷil 15 ilaṭcam taṉi iṉaṅkaḷ (Species) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭup peyar cūṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum iṉam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭātavaiyum, peyar cūṭṭappaṭātavaiyum (kuṟippāka nilaittiṇai, nuṇ ṇuyirkaḷ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷ) mummaṭaṅku irukkum eṉṟu aṟiviya lārkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟaikku 24 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar oru taṭavaiyum/, āṟaraik kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar maṟṟoru taṭavaiyum pēraḻivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu antantak kālakkaṭṭattil irunta uyiriṉaṅkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavai aḻintuviṭṭaṉa. (mikap periya viṇ koḷḷikaḷ (Meteors) ulakil viḻuntatāl appēraḻivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar). ippoḻutu uḷḷa pālūṭṭikaḷ (Mammals) aṉaittumē 16 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉa oru ciṟiya (eli pōṉṟa parumaṉuṭaiya) vilaṅkiliruntu paṭimalarcci aṭaintavaiyē. kuraṅkukaḷukkum māntaṉukkum mūtātai yāṉa ilemūr (Lemur) vilaṅku (ēṟattāḻa 250 kirām eṭai) uruvāṉa kālam āṟaraik kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ākum. (pūccikaḷ tōṉṟi 50 kōṭi āṇṭum eṟumpukaḷ tēṉīkkaḷ tōṉṟi 10 kōṭi āṇṭum, karaiyāṉ tōṉṟi 28 kōṭi āṇṭum ākiṉṟaṉa) ippakkattiṟkum muṉ pakkattukkum iṭaiyil oṭṭiyuḷḷa “puṭaviyiṉ tōṟṟattiliruntu ikkāla māntaṉ varai” eṉṟa vivarap paṭattai kāṇka.

2. māntaṉ eppaṭit tōṉṟiṉāṉ? māntak kuraṅkiṉat tukkum (cimpaṉci, korillā) māntaṉukkum potuvāṉa vēṟu ōr uyiriṉam iṉṟaikku ēṟattāḻa 50 ilakkam āṇ ṭukku muṉ āppirikkāvil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiviyal muṭivu. iṉṟu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa 650 kōṭi māntarkaḷumē (atāvatu tirāviṭar, intō airōppiyar, maṅkōliyar, cemittiyar, amerikka intiyar ākiya aṉaivarumē) hōmō cēpiyaṉs cēpiyaṉs allatu taṟkāla māntar eṉṟa (Home Sapiens Sapiens or Anatomically Modern Humans) eṉṉum orē iṉattaic cārntavarkaḷ. ivviṉam āppirikkāvil iṉṟaikku oṉṟarai ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vāḻnta orē tāyiṭam iruntu tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟaiya aṟiviyalār aṉaivarum ēṟṟa muṭivu (Cavalli Sforza and others: The history and geography of Human genes, 1994). ēṟattāḻa māntaṉaiyotta “muṉmānta (Hominid)” iṉaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 48 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷil tōṉṟic cila pala ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷ vāḻntu piṉṉar muṟṟilum aḻintoḻintu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ hōmō erakṭas eṉṟa iṉamum aṭaṅkum. atu maṭṭumē āppirik kāvai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷilum paraviyiruntatu. kurōmakṉāṉ maṉitaṉ, pīkiṅ māntaṉ, cāvaka māntaṉ, atti rampākkam pācil māntaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ (ēṟattāḻa 3 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vāḻntavarkaḷ) inta hōmō erakṭs vakaiyaic cārntavarkaḷē. airōppāvil 40,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ varai vāḻntu piṉṉar aṭiyōṭu aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭa niyāṉṭartal (Neandarthal) iṉamum muṉmānta iṉamē. inta “muṉmānta iṉaṅkaḷ” evaṟ ṟiṭaiyēyum “moḻi” uruvākavē illai (None of them had the faculty of speech)

		3. ippoḻutuḷḷa māntarkaḷākiya  AMH vakaiyaic cārnta nam mānta iṉam, kaṭanta oṉṟarai ilaṭcam āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil tōṉṟi ulakeṅkum piṉ varumāṟu paraviyatu - atāvatu,

caipīriyāvukku 		iṉṟaikku 	30,000 		āṇṭukaḷukku
										muṉṉarum
airōppāvukku			 ” 	40,000 			”
vaṭa/teṉ
amerikkāvukku 			” 	30,000 - 12,000		”
āttirēliyāvukku 			” 	50,000 			”
cappāṉukku 				” 	30,000 			”

pacipik tīvukaḷukku 			” 	4000 - 1000 			”
(maikrōṉiciyā, pāliṉīciyā)

paraviṉar eṉpatu vallunar karuttu ākum. itai viḷakkum nilappaṭaṅkaḷ aṭutta iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. kaṇṭaṅkaḷ nakarvuk koḷkaiyiṉ paṭi  (Continental drift) kaṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 25 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷākap pirintu nakarntuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum ulakil iṉṟu uḷḷa kaṇṭaṅkaḷ ellām ēṟattāḻa ippōtuḷḷa uruvai ēṟattāḻa oru kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē aṭaintuviṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup piṉṉar, kaṇṭam aḷavukku (Continental proportions) peru nilappakuti etuvum kaṭalukkuḷ mūḻkavillai. āyiṉum paṉiyūḻi muṭivil ki.mu. 8000 vākkil (paṉikkaṭṭi uruki kaṭal maṭṭam cumār 300 aṭi uyarntatāl ulakeṅkum kaṭalōrap pakutiyākiya kaṇṭattiṭṭu (Continental shelf) ēṟattāḻa - irunūṟu kal aḷavukku kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyatu. avvāṟu akkālak kaṭṭattil tamiḻakattaic cuṟṟiyum (teṉ ticai uṭpaṭa) cila nūṟu kal (mail) tolaivum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyirukkalām. avvāṟu mūḻkiya nilappakutiyaiyē kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kaḷaviyal uraiyum cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē iṉṟaiya aṟiviyalukkup poruntuva tākum. āppirikkāviliruntu taṟkāla māntaṉ ēṟattāḻa 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar āttirēliyā, mēṟku āciyā pōṉṟa pakutikaḷukku pulam  yarntu ceṉṟatu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāka irukkalām. jiyākirāpikal mākacīṉ cepṭempar 2006 itaḻil taṟkālamāntar āppirikkāvai viṭṭut teṉṉintiyāvaiyoṭṭiya kaṇṭattiṭṭu vaḻiyākap pulampeyarntu ceṉṟa pātaiyaik kāṭṭum nilappaṭam uḷḷatu. akkālak kaṭṭattil ippoḻutu uḷḷatu pōlavē ellāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa veṉiṉum nilappakuti cila nūṟumail virivāka iruntirukkum. intiyak karai cārnta kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyil atāvatu karaiyōrak kaṭaṟpakutiyil (Continental shelf)āḻkaṭal akaḻāyvu ceytāl itu paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaikkalām eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār piḷemiṅ (2004). iṉṟaiya māntaṉiṭam uḷḷavai 23x2 ‘kurōmōcōm’kaḷ. avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya marapaṇukkaḷ (Genomes) eṇṇikkai 30,000. namatu marapaṇukkaḷukkum cimpaṉci marapaṇukkaḷukkum 98.50 viḻukkāṭu oṟṟumai uṇṭu. (oṉṟarai viḻukkāṭē marapaṇu nilaiyil cimpaṉciyiliruntu māntaṉ vēṟupaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟālum māntaṉ evvaḷavu māperum koṭumaiyāṉa uyiriyāka māṟi viṭṭāṉ!).



Images from page nos 16 & 17
		4. kaṇṭaṅkaḷ nakarvukkoḷkai, maṟṟum kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷ kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya ceyti ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil kaḻaka nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇum kaṭalkōḷ ceyti kaḷai iṉṟaiya aṟiviyalukkēṟpa viḷakka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. kumari muṉaikkut teṟkil nilappakutikaḷ kaṭalkōḷil mūḻkiya ceytiyaik kalittokai 104m cilappatikāram kāṭu kāṇkātaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyattiṉ mutal uraikārar ākiya (ki.pi.10ām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cārnta) iḷampūraṇarum itaik kuṟippiṭukiṟār. avarukkup piṉ ṉar vanta iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ uraiyāciriyarum
aṭiyārkku nallārum icceytiyai mēlum virivupaṭutti caṟṟu mikaippaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyam kūṟum ikkaṭalkōḷ ceytiyaip paṟṟi eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa āyvuraikaḷ varumāṟu:

ca.cōmacuntarapārati (1913) tamiḻp paṇṭai ilakkiyaṅ kaḷum tamiḻakamum, cittānta tīpikā XIV
vi.jē. tampi piḷḷai (1913) : māṇikkavācakar toṉma varalāṟu, tamiḻiyaṉ āṇṭikuvāri II – 1
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1930) : māṇikkavācakar varalāṟum kālamum
ē.es. vaittiyanāta aiyar (1929) : “kīḻaināṭukaḷiṉ piraḷayat toṉmaṅkaḷ” : pampāy varalāṟṟuk kaḻaka jarṉal II – 1
jē. periyanāyakam (1941) maṉuviṉ piraḷayam : ti niyū riviyū XI
hīrās pātiriyār (1954) tol intō naṇṇilak karai nākarika āyvukaḷ iyal IV pak. 411-439 catapatapirāmaṇam 1,8 mutaliyavaṟṟil kūṟappaṭum “maṉu piraḷayam” tirāviṭat toṉmattiliruntu uruvākiyatu eṉa (iii) um iv) um kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. cumeriyap piraḷayak kataikūṭap paḻantamiḻk kaṭalkōḷ toṉmattiliruntu uruppeṟṟatē eṉa (i) m vi)m karutukiṉṟaṉa.

5. intiyamākkaṭalil paḻaṅkālattil oru kaṇṭam iruntu atu kaṭalil mūḻki viṭṭatu eṉṟu 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu iṟutiyil hēkkalum vēṟu cilarum karuti ataṟku “lemūriyā” eṉṟa peyaraiyum iṭṭaṉar. āṉāl iṉṟuḷḷa aṟiviya laṟiñarkaḷiṉ orumitta karuttu eṉṉa? kaṇṭam aḷavukkup periya nilap parappu etuvum entak kālattilum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṭa villai eṉpatē avarkaḷ muṭivu eṉak kaṇṭōm. kāntira tāv tamatu “mukkaṭaṟputirkaḷ” nūlil itai 1974lēyē cuṭṭiyuḷḷār. eṉiṉum itai yārum kaṇṭukoḷvatillai! 

“toṉmai nākarikaṅkaḷaip paṭaitta mānta iṉaṅkaḷaik koṇṭirunta (kaṇṭam pōṉṟa) peru nilapparappukaḷ evaiyum entak kālattilum intiya, pacipik, aṭlāṇṭik peruṅkaṭalkaḷ evaṟṟilum iruntiruppataṟkāṉa vāyppu aṟavē illai eṉalām”.

		“It is highly improbable that big land masses, inhabited by large numbers of people who created ancient civilizations ever existed in the Indian, Pacific or Atlantic Oceans”. (p267)

ippoḻutu aṟiviyal ēṟṟuḷḷatu kaṇṭa nakarvu maṟṟum pūmippāḷaṅkaḷ kōṭpāṭē (Continental drift and Plate techtonics)yākum. itaṉai viḷakkum ulakap paṭaṅkaḷ iraṇṭai aṭuttapakkaṅkaḷil kāṇka. āyiṉum ki.mu. 8,000ai oṭṭi urm (WURM) paṉiyūḻi iṟutiyil kaṭalmaṭṭam uyarntu ulakeṅkum ēṟattāḻa irunūṟu, munnūṟu kal aḷavukku kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti kaṭalil mūḻkiviṭṭatu eṉpatai ipputiya kōṭpāṭum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. akkaṭalkōḷ kālattiṟku muṉṉar intup peruṅkaṭalil uḷḷa tīvukaḷ mūlamāka āppirikkāvaiyum teṉṉintiyāvaiyum iṇaitta vāl pōṉṟa nilappakutikaḷum cila (piṉṉar mūḻki viṭṭavai) iruntirukkalām eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. (vālṭar pērcarvīs; The Roots of Ancient India, 1971).

6. ilemūriyāk kaṇṭak koḷkai nilavi vanta kālattilum kūṭa ca. cōmacuntarapārati, mayilai cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi, em.ārōkkiyacāmi pōṉṟa ciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷ kumarikkut teṟkil kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya pakuti kaṇṭam aḷaviṉa taṉṟu; ciṟu nilappakutiyāka irukkalām eṉṟē kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷaṉar. kā.appātturaiyārum ‘kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭatai uṇarka. ippoḻutu ilemūriyāk kaṇṭak koḷkai ātāramaṟṟatu eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭu viṭṭataic cākkāka vaittuk koṇṭu cumati irāmacāmi taṉatu kaṟpaṉaip puviyiyalāḷarkaḷum, pēraḻivu varalāṟu kaḷum; mūḻkiviṭṭa ilemūriyā : Fabulous geographers, catastrophic Histories : The Lost Lemuria (2004 : permaṉeṉṭ piḷāk, ṭelli) eṉṉum nūlil “kaṭalkoṇṭa teṉṉāṭu” paṟṟi eḻutiya tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ aṉaivaraiyum tākki ēḷaṉam ceytuḷḷār. 1950kaḷukkup piṉ “lemūriyāk kaṇṭak” koḷkaiyai aṟiviyal aṟavē kaiviṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatu uṇmaitāṉ. āṉāl cumati irāmacāmi oṉṟaik kuṟippiṭa maṟukkiṟār. atu eṉṉa? kalittokai, cilappatikāram, piṟkāla uraiyāciriyar nūlkaḷ cuṭṭum kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu ciṟiya aḷaviṉatāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟu tamiḻaka aracu 1972il veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu – tol paḻaṅkālam pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvatai avar kaṇṭu koḷḷavillai. aṉṟaiya ‘nāṭu’ eṉpatu ‘intiyā’ ‘tamiḻ
nāṭu’ pōṉṟa perunilap pakutiyaṉṟu. iṉṟaiya vaṭṭa (tālukā) aḷaviṟkuḷ kūṭa iraṇṭu - mūṉṟu nāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavē. uraiyāciriyarkaḷ kuṟitta 49 nāṭukaḷum cērntu kaṇṭam aḷavukku iruntirukka vēṇṭiyatēyillai. muṉ pattiyil kūṟiyatu pōla ki.mu. 8,000ai oṭṭi kaṇṭattiṭṭuppakuti kaṭalil mūḻkiyataiyē kaḻaka nūlkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ ceytikaḷ kūṟuvatākak koṇṭāl iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷukkuk kaḻaka (caṅka) ilakkiyak kaṭalkōḷ ceyti muṟṟilum icaivatēyākum eṉpatu tēṟṟam. (kāṇka pi.irāmanātaṉ 1998,2003,2004).

Images from page nos  20 & 21
		7. ivvāṟu tamiḻ, tamiḻar ceytikaḷaik koccaip paṭutti eḻutuvatu ivvāciriyarukku vaḻakkamāṉatē. ti intiyaṉ ekaṉāmik aṇṭ cōciyal hisṭari riviyū 38-2 ēpral - cūṉ 2001 itaḻil cumati irāmacāmi “iṉaṅkaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷ : tolliyal (tamiḻt) tēciya uṇarvu, cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉpāl īrppu” eṉṟu oru kaṭṭurai eḻutiṉār. cintu veḷi nākarikamum annākarika muttirai eḻuttukaḷum tirāviṭam (toltamiḻ) cārntavaiyē eṉṟu ciṟanta āyvāḷarkaḷ kamil cuvelapil (1990: tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal - ōr aṟimukam, iyal 6 : tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum hārappā moḻiyum) askō parpōlā (praṇṭ laiṉ ak 24,2000) karutuvataiyellām ak kaṭṭuraiyil cumati irāmacāmi kaṇṭu koḷḷavillai. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ tamiḻac cārpai valiyuṟuttum tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷai yellām (parpōlā, pārcarvīs, cuvalepil pōṉṟavarkaḷ āyvukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil eḻutupavarkaḷ uṭpaṭa), avar eḷḷi nakaiyāṭiyuḷḷār.

8. ippakutiyil iṟutiyāka aṟiviyal cārnta iraṇṭu ceytikaḷait taruvatu poruttamākum. avai tamiḻar varalāṟu paṟṟiyavaiyalla. eṉiṉum māntaṉ ovvoruvaṉum terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyavai. oru ceyti aṇṭattaip paṟṟiyatu. maṟṟatu piṇṭattaip paṟṟiyatu.
9. mutalāvatu pirapañcattōṟṟattaip paṟṟiyatu. ataṉai āṉanta vikaṭaṉ 10.9.2006 itaḻil aṟiñar cujātā viḷakkiyatu pōl teḷivāka yārālum viḷakka iyalātu. ataṉaik kīḻē kāṇka:

“vikaṭaṉ 6.8.06 itaḻil, ‘pirapañcattil aṇukkaḷukku aḻivillai’ eṉṉum pil praicaṉiṉ karuttaik kuṟippiṭṭōm.

praicaṉ, pirapañcattiṉ ārampa kaṇattai vivarittu eḻutiyirukkiṟār. Big bang eṉṟu avar collum ārampa veṭit tattuvam ippōtu aṟiviyalāḷarkaḷāl potuvāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭu irukkiṟatu.

oru purōṭṭāṉ eṉpatu ettaṉai ciṟiyatu? ataṉ ‘ṭ’ṭaṉṉāviṉ puḷḷiyiṉ parimāṇattil 500,000,000,000 purōṭṭāṉkaḷai aṭakka muṭiyum. allatu aintu laṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku ettaṉai cekaṇṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavō, ēṟattāḻa attaṉai purōṭṭāṉkaḷai aṭakka muṭiyum.

		inta purōṭṭāṉai 100 kōṭi pākamākkiṉāl ēṟpaṭum avvaḷavu ciṟiya iṭaiveḷiyil, oru avuṉs mēṭṭar eṉṉum parupporuḷai aṭaittu pāruṅkaḷ... nīṅkaḷ oru pirapañcattai ciruṣṭikkat tayār! parimāṇamaṟṟa attaṉai ciṟiya iṭattil pirapañcattiṉ attaṉai parupporuḷkaḷiṉ cāttiyaṅkaḷum aṭarntirunta anta ārampa kaṇattai ‘orumitta kaṇam’ (ciṅkulāriṭṭi - singularity) eṉkiṟārkaḷ. atiliruntu, kāraṇamiṉṟi tiṭīreṉa attaṉai caktiyum veṭittuc citaṟip paraviyatu.

itait taḷḷi niṉṟu yārum pārttirukka muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, taḷḷi niṟka iṭam etuvum iruntirukkātu. ‘spēs’ eṉṉum viṇveḷiyē antac citaṟaliṉpōtu uṇṭāṉatu tāṉ. antak kaṇattukku muṉ eṉṉa iruntatu eṉṟum colla muṭiyātu. kāraṇamum, kālamum appōtutāṉ tuvaṅkiyatu!

maṉacil eṇṇip pārkkak kaṣṭamāka irukkiṟatallavā? nammāḻvār muyaṟci ceytirukkiṟār. ‘oṉṟum tēvum ulakum uyirum maṟṟum yātumillā aṉṟu...’ pirapañcam appaṭit tāṉ ārampittatu eṉkiṟatu kāsmālaji. ‘oṉṟum illai’yil iruntu ‘ellām uṇṭu’ eṉkiṟa māṟutal nikaḻnta oru mantira upaupaupa kaṇattiṉ piriviltāṉ īrppu vicaikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iyaṟpiyal vitikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
mutal nimiṣattil pirapañcam pala kōṭi nūṟāyiram mailkaḷ paraviviṭṭatu. iṉṟuvarai virivaṭaintukoṇṭē irukkiṟatu. anta pilliyaṉ ṭikiri uṣṇattil, mutalil haiṭrajaṉ, hīliyam pōṉṟa eḷiya taṉimaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.

ārampitta mūṉṟu nimiṭaṅkaḷil maṟṟa attaṉai taṉimaṅkaḷum uṇṭākiviṭṭaṉa. ivai tāṉ pirapañcattil iṉṟum puḻakkattil uḷḷaṉa. etaṟkum aḻivē kiṭaiyātu. oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟāka māṟuvatutāṉ, parupporuḷ caktiyāka, cakti parupporuḷāka... itaiyellām eppaṭik kaṇṭupiṭit tārkaḷ? pakkattil iruntu pārttatu pōlac col kiṟārkaḷē, eppaṭi? 

anta ārampa kaṇattil iṉṟaiya viḷaivukaḷ, viṇ veḷiyil eṅku nōkkiṉum oru kāsmik maikrōvēv piṉpula veḷiccamākat terikiṟatu. naṭcattiraṅkaḷ aṉaittum vilaki vilakic celvataṟku attāṭciyāka viṇveḷiyil tūrattu naṭcattiraṅkaḷiṉ oḷiyai spekṭrāskōp mūlam pārkkumpōtu, reṭ ṣiḵpṭ eṉṟa niṟa mālaiyiṉ civappu niṟa iṭamāṟṟam iruppatu terikiṟatu. itiliruntu ārampa kaṇattaik kaṇakkiṭṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟaikku ēṟattāḻa 13.7 pilliyaṉ (200 milliyaṉ muṉṉē piṉṉē irukkalām) āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nikaḻntatām. (pilliyaṉ eṉpatu 100 kōṭi; milliyaṉ 10 laṭcam)”

10. (i) iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa 600 kōṭi māntar aṉaivarumē ēṟattāḻa oṉṟarai ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil vāḻnta oru peṟṟōr allatu kuḻuviṉariṭamiruntu tāṉ uruvākiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpatai iṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ eppaṭi niṟuviyuḷḷaṉar? ovvoru maṉitaṉiṉ uṭalil uḷḷa ticu (cel)kaḷ eṇṇikkai 100 ṭirilliyaṉ (= 1 kōṭi kōṭikaḷ = 1014). ivaṟṟuḷ irattac civappaṇuk kaḷākiya celkaḷ tavirap piṟa ovvoṉṟukkuḷḷum oru maiyakkūṟu nucleus uḷḷatu. akkūṟu ovvoṉṟukkuḷḷum DNA  eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum 23 iṇai kurōmocōmkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 22 iṇaikaḷ āṇukkum peṇṇukkum potuvāṉavai. 23vatu iṇai peṇṇukku X, X  eṉṟum āṇukku x, y eṉṟum irukkum. y kurōmōcōm āṇ vaḻiyākavē varum. ovvoru cellukkuḷḷum (ataṟkuḷ irattattiṉ mūlam vantucērum kuḷukōs, uyirvaḷi, pōṉṟavaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭutti) vētiyiyal ceyalpāṭukaḷai nikaḻttum - energy metabolism - maiṭakāṇṭiriyā (mitochondria) eṉṉum mīcciṟu amaippukaḷum pala uḷḷaṉa. maiṭakāṉṭiri yāvukku ‘celliṉ DNA’ viliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉi DNA uṇṭu. āṇ vintaṇuvum peṇ karu muṭṭaiyum cērntu māntak karu uruvākumpoḻutu peṇ karuviliruntu maṭṭumē maiṭakāṉṭiriyākkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaru muṭṭaiyaiviṭa mika nuṇṇiyatāṉa āṇ vintaṇuvil maiṭakāṇṭiriyā iruppataṟku iṭamēyillai. eṉavē tāyvaḻiyākavē maiṭakāṭiriyākaḷai avaḷ makaḷkaḷum makaṉkaḷum peṟuvar. āṉāl makaḷkaḷiṭamiruntu tāṉ aṭutta talaimuṟaikku maiṭakāṉṭiriyā cella iyalum. talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka maiṭakāṇṭiriyākkaḷ tāy - makaḷ eṉṟavāṟu iṟaṅki varumpoḻutu ovvoru talaimuṟaiyilum maiṭakāṉṭiriyā DNA amaippu ettaṉai viḻukkāṭu māṟutal aṭaiyum eṉpatai aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭittuviṭṭatu. āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku enta aḷavu mai DNA māṟṟamaṭaiyum eṉṟu nirṇayikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatāl akkaṇakkiṉpaṭi piṉṉōkkic ceṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ iṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka kaṇṭupiṭittu niṟuviyuḷḷatu eṉṉa? iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa aṉaittu māntarkaḷumē cumār 1,50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil vāḻntuvanta oru iṇaiyar allatu oru kuḻuviṉariṉ piṟaṅ kaṭaikaḷ eṉpatē atu. “ellōrum ōriṉam”. [cellil uḷḷa DNA vil 23vatu kurōmōcōmil āṇkaḷāyiṉ uḷḷa la kurōmōcōm māṟṟam aṭaiyum vikitattaik kaṇṭupiṭittu ammāṟṟa vikitattaip piṉṉōkkik kaṇakkiṭṭuc ceṉṟa poḻutu kiṭaitta muṭivum maiṭakāṉṭiriyā DNA aṭippaṭaiyil kiṭaitta muṭivaic cariyeṉṟu meyppittuḷḷatu]

(ii) mēṟkuṟittu āyvukaḷiliruntu viññāṉikaḷ mēlum niṟuviyuḷḷatu: iṉṟaikku 70,000-50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarttāṉ āppirikkāvai viṭṭu taṟkāla mānta iṉam pulampeyarntu ulakiṉ piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷukkup paraviyatu eṉpatākum. avvāṟu āppirikkāvai viṭṭu peyarntu paraviya māntap piriviṉaril mukkiyamāṉa oru pirivu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyākac (teṉṉintiyā eṉpatu akkāla kaṭṭattil kaṭalōra nilappakutiyāka irunta kaṇṭattiṭṭu Continental shelf  pakutiyum cērntatu) ceṉṟu cilar aṅkēyē nilaittiṭa vēṟu cilar āstirēliyā varai kaṭalōra nilappakuti vaḻiyāka metu metuvāka atāvatu oru talaimuṟaikku oṉṟiraṇṭu mail tolaivu vītam) payaṇittu ceṉṟaṉar. (akkaṇṭattil) āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eccaṅkaḷ ki.mu. 50000 mutal uḷḷaṉa.

 (iii) iṉṟaiya tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ avvāṟu iṉṟaikku 70000-50000 āṇṭu muṉṉar āppirikkāvai viṭṭu nīṅki āstirēliyāvaraic ceṉṟaṭainta māntariṉ piṟaṅkaṭaikaḷ perumaḷavil uḷḷaṉar eṉpatai mai DNA āyviṉ mūlam niṟuviyuḷḷavar maturai kāmarācar kalaik kaḻaka aṟiviyalaṟiñar ṭākṭar irāmacāmi piccappaṉ āvār. 23.8.2007, 16.4.2008 tētikaḷil avar ceytittāḷkaḷukku nērkāṇalkaḷil terivitta ceytikaḷ varumāṟu 

“āppirikkāvai viṭṭu iṉṟaikku 70000-50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar veḷiyēṟiya oru kuḻuviṉariṭam mai DNA  aṭaiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭu M130 tōṉṟiyatu. iṉṟuḷḷa āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ tokaiyiṉaruḷ iraṇṭil oruvariṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu.

“maturaip pakutiyil uḷḷa makkaḷ palar mai DNA  vai āyvu ceytatil avarkaḷil 5rūliruntu 7% naparkaḷiṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu eṉṟu teriya vantatu. aṉṟaiya kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti vaḻiyāka mānta iṉattiṉ oru pirivu āppirikkāvi liruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pulam peyarntatu eṉpatai inta āyvu meyppittatu (Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences; USA; 2001) piriṭṭaṉ nāṭṭu marapaṇuviyalaṟiñar car
vālṭar pāṭmar coṉṉatu pōl. “nām aṉaivarum āppirikkāviliruntu pulam peyarntu vantavarkaḷiṉ piṟaṅkaṭaikaḷē” (inta M130 ai piccappaṉ mutalil kaṇṭu piṭittatu ucilampaṭṭi arukē jōti māṇikkam kirāmam āṇṭit tēvar makaṉ tiru virumāṇṭi avarkaḷiṭam tāṉ)

“intiya makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum mai DNA aṭaiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭukaḷil mikat toṉmai vāyntatu M130 tāṉ. ataiviṭap paḻaimaiyāṉa mai DNA  kuṟiyīṭukaḷ intiyāvil evariṭamum illai. virumāṇṭiyiṭamum vēṟu cilariṭam kaṇṭa inta M130 kuṟiyīṭu niṟuviyuḷḷatu eṉṉa?  āppirikkāvil ikkāla mānta iṉam AMH  uruvākiya piṉṉar akkaṇṭattai viṭṭu mutalil pulam peyarntu intiyāvil mutalil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷiṉ piṟaṅkaṭaikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷaṉar eṉpatē atu.”

ca. moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum

muṉmānta iṉaṅkaḷ aṉaittum pēccāṟṟal aṟ ṟavai. avarkaḷiṭaiyē moḻi uruvākavillai, ēṟattāḻa 11/2 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉāl tōṉṟiya taṟkāla mānta iṉattiṭaiyē (AMH) maṭṭumē ēṟattāḻa 50000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar moḻi uruvākiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟaiya palatuṟai aṟiviyalāḷariṉ orumitta muṭivākum.

iṉṟu piṉvarum vivarappaṭi ēṟattāḻa 6000 moḻikaḷ ulakil pēcappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa:

(inti, maitili, rājastāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟai orē inti moḻiyākak koḷvatā? taṉittaṉi moḻikaḷākak koḷvatā? itu paṟṟi ellām moḻiyiyalāḷariṭam karuttu vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. 6000 allatu 6059 eṉpatellām oru
kuttumatippēyākum.) inta 6059 moḻikaḷil 4 viḻukkāṭu ākiya 242(8+72+162) moḻikaḷaip pēcuvōr eṇṇikkai yāṉa 576 kōṭiyāṉatu mottam 600 kōṭi makkaḷuḷ 95 viḻukkāṭu ākum. mīti 96 viḻukkāṭu ākiya 5817 moḻikaḷaip pēcuvōr veṟum 24 kōṭi makkaḷēyāvar (aṟunūṟu kōṭiyil avarkaḷ veṟum nāṉku viḻukkāṭṭiṉarēyāvar).

2. iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa moḻikaḷ aṉaittum orē mutaṉ moḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyirukka vāyppuṇṭu eṉṟa koḷkai 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ māksmullarukkum icai vāṉatē. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ñāla mutaṉ moḻi āyvil īṭupaṭṭa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ petarcaṉ, tirāmpetti, cuvā tecu, kirīṉperk, meriṭ rūlaṉ, illic cuvitic, tāl kōpālski, cevarōskiṉ, stārōstiṉ, pāmhārṭu maṟṟum kerṉs, jāṉ peṅṭcaṉ, vāklāv pilācak pōṉṟa palarāvar. palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷaiyum piṉvarumāṟu oru cila peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkuḷ (Super families) aṭakkalām eṉpatu avarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷa uṇmaiyākum.
i) nāstirāṭik (intō-airōppiyaṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, urālik, altāyk, kārtvelliyaṉ, āprō- ēciyāṭik-atāvatu cemittiya-hāmittiyak kuṭumpam, ākiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ itil aṭaṅkum). kirīṉperk vakuttuḷḷa yūrēciyāṭik peruṅkuṭumpattukkum itaṟkum atika vēṟupāṭu illai. yūrēciyāṭik-kil aṭaṅkiyavai etrus kaṉ, intō-airōppiyaṉ, urālik-yūkākīr, altāyk, koriyaṉ-cappāṉiyam-aiṉu, kilyāk, cukōtiyaṉ, eskimōalyūt ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷākum.

(2000 akṭōparil sṭāṉpōrṭu palkalaikkaḻakattil patri rājū kiruṣṇamūrttiyai kirīṉperk cantitta poḻutu, “tirāviṭa moḻi yūrēciyāṭik peruṅkuṭumpattiṉ cakōtari yāka irukkalām. makaḷāka irukka muṭiyātu” eṉat terivit tuḷḷār (Bh. Krishnamurthi. The Dravidian Languages; 2003, pak.46). itiliruntu slāv, irāṉiyaṉ, vētamoḻi/ camas kirutam, kirīk, ilattīṉ, keltik, cerumāṉiyam pōṉṟa moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkellām tāyāṉa intō airōp piyattai viṭat toṉmai vāyntatu tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam, atāvatu paḻantamiḻ eṉṟu uṇaralām.)

		ii. cīṉa-kākēciyaṉ
		iii. āstirik (muṇṭā pōṉṟavai)
		iv. amerikka intiya moḻikaḷ
		v. intō-pacipik
		vi. kōycāṉ
		vii. kāṅkō-cakārā

palvēṟu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷai iṇaittu avaṟṟukku mūlamāṉa moḻip peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷaik kāṇum āyvāḷar kaḷuḷ cilar ataṟkum mēlē pōy ñāla mutaṉmoḻi (māntaṉiṉ mutal tāymoḻi) āyvukkum ceṉṟuḷḷaṉar. meriṭ rūlaṉ taṉatu moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟam-moḻikaḷiṉ koṭivaḻi āyvu (sṭāṉpōrṭu palkalaikkaḻakam, 1994) nūliṉ pakkam 277 il kūṟuvatu varumāṟu: “perumpālāṉa moḻiyiyalaṟiñarkaḷ ottukkoḷḷāviṭiṉum allatu aiyappāṭṭuṭaṉ karutiṉum iṉṟuḷḷa moḻikaḷ aṉaittum  (iṉṟu iṟantuviṭṭa āṉāl muṉṉar nilaviyataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭiyuḷḷa moḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa) orē ñāla mutaṉ moḻiyiliruntu tāṉ tōṉṟiṉa eṉpataṟku valuvāṉa ātāram uḷḷatu” annūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 277-366il 27 mukkiyamāṉa karuttukaḷukkup palvēṟu moḻikkuṭumpaṅ kaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷ “Global Etymologies” tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akkaruttukkaḷukku ñāla mutaṉ moḻiyil eṉṉa vērc col iruntirukkalām eṉṟu ūkikka atu muṉṉōṭi yākum. avaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟukku (ēṟattāḻa 17kku)t tamiḻc coṟkaḷē poruḷ potintaṉavākavum ñālamutaṉ moḻiyiṉ vērccol vaṭivai viḷakkuvaṉavākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa.

3. irāparṭ kālṭuvel 1856il taṉatu tirāviṭa atāvatu teṉ intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam eṉṉum māperum nūlai veḷiyiṭṭār. (tiruntiya virivāṉa iraṇṭām patippu 1875) oru puṟam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum maṟupuṟam,

		intō - airōppiya moḻikaḷ
		cemittiya moḻikaḷ
		elāmaiṭ moḻi
		cittiya (ippoḻutu ‘urāl-alṭāyk’) moḻikaḷ
		cappāṉiya moḻi
		āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷ

ākiyavaṟṟukkum iṭaiyē kāṇum ilakkaṇa oppumaikaḷ, col oppumaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa ceyti kaḷai avar tantār. immoḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ piriyumuṉṉar irunta (māntaṉ toṉ moḻiyiṉ) nilaiyai viḷakkuvataṟkāṉa oḷiyai ñāla mutaṉ moḻikku mika neruṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārnta moḻikaḷiliruntutāṉ peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉpatai acceytikaḷ valuvāka niṟuviṉa. kālṭuvel kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyatai mēlum nuṇuki ārāyntu “mānta iṉa mutaṉ moḻi oṉṟākattāṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum; mikat toṉmai vāynta toltamiḻē (toltirāviṭam eṉa aḻaikka virumpuvār avvāṟē aḻaikkalām, maṟuppillai) aṉaittu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkum (intō-āriya moḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa) mūlamāṉa moḻiyākak karutappaṭa vēṇṭum” eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭai mēlum āḻamāka virivāka niṟuviyavarkaḷ nallūr cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācarum ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum āvar. intō airōppiya moḻikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷiṉ (ēṉ piṟamoḻikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷukkum tāṉ) aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟukku ñāṉappirakācarum pāvāṇarum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa poruttamāṉa tol-tirāviṭa vērc coṟkaḷai iṉam kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. 1953il ñāṉappirakācar terivitta karuttu varumāṟu:-

				“intō airōppiya moḻikaḷil ‘vēr kaḷ’ eṉa uṉṉikkappaṭupavai poruḷ toṭar paṟṟa veṟum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tām. tirāviṭa moḻi vērkaḷōveṉil aiyattiṟkiṭa miṉṟip poruḷai yum kāraṇattaiyum kāṭṭupavaiyāka uyirōṭṭa muḷḷa muḷaikkaru pōlat tōṉṟum; entak karuttōṭṭattil palvēṟu coṟkaḷ uruvāyiṉa eṉpatait teṟṟeṉa avai kāṭṭum vakaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. ittaṉmai avaṟṟukku il lātatāl intō airōppiya “vērkaḷ” iruḷ maṇṭiya poruḷaṟṟa veṟum olik kuviyalkaḷā kavē tōṉṟum. avaṟṟukkum teḷivu tantu oḷi kāṭṭi uyirūṭṭam tarakkūṭiyavai tirāviṭamoḻi vērkaḷtām.”

piṟamoḻi vērkaḷukkum ikkaruttu perum aḷavukkup poruntuvatākum. ceṉṉaik kiṟistuvak kallūri itaḻil  (1929) ñāṉappirakācar “moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟam” eṉṟa kaṭṭurai yil “intō airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ tantaiyākiya toṉ moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷ tamiḻil tāṉ kiṭṭum” eṉa muḻaṅkiyiruntār. (Tamil supplies this long looked for clue to finding the true origin of the proto Indo – European language) 

4. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum piṟa moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa uṟavukaḷ kuṟittu ituvarai piṉvarum āyvukaḷ nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa:

5. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa mika neruṅkiya oppumaiyai pi.irāmanātaṉ (kuppam) tirāviṭap palkalaik kaḻakattiṉ tirāviṭiyaṉ sṭaṭīs 1-3; ēpral - cūṉ 2003 itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭurai virivāka niṟuvukiṟatu. tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉintiyāvil kaṇṭippāka 10,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē iruntu irukka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum iṅkiruntu ataṟku muṉṉarē āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tol tamiḻ makkaḷiṭamiruntu pirintu ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum akkaṭṭurai niṟuvukiṟatu.

ru. tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paraviyatu
teṟkiliruntu vaṭakku nōkkiyē yākum;

tamiḻar intiyāviṉ tolkuṭikaḷ

		1. ippoḻutu pala nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa paltuṟai aṟiñar kaḷum potuvāka ēṟṟuk kaikkoṇṭuḷḷa (tavaṟāṉa) karutu kōḷiṉpaṭi toltirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar tāyakam vaṭa kiḻakku īrāṉ pakutiyākum; aṅkiruntu cumār ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭi avarkaḷ intiyā, teṉṉintiyā, ilaṅkaiyai nōkki atāvatu teṟku nōkki iṭam peyarntu ceṉṟaṉar; cellum vaḻiyil, pēruntu varumpoḻutu āṅkāṅku cilar iṟakki viṭappaṭuvatu pōla, tirāviṭa moḻipēcum kuḻukkaḷ cila āṅkāṅku viṭṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. (kē.vi. cuvalepil (1972): “tirāviṭarkaḷ iṟakkam” The descent of the Dravidians; tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal paṟṟiya paṉṉāṭṭu āyvitaḻ (IJDL) tokuti 2; pak. 57-63. pirittāṉiyak kalaik kaḷañciyattil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ paṟṟi avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭṭuraiyilum avar ik karuttaiyē kūṟukiṟār. tamatu teṉṉintiya varalāṟu (4m patippu 1976) nūlil kē.ē.nīlakaṇṭa cāstiriyum itu “naṭan tirukkak kūṭātatu alla” (not unlikely) eṉkiṟār.

2. itaṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷkai tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar vaṭakku nōkkip paraviṉar eṉṉum “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” koḷkaiyākum: DRAVIDIAN ASCENT (from south). ippoḻutu atu paḻaiya pāṇik koḷkaiyākak karutap paṭiṉum ataṉai valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷuḷ her. ār. hāl; hīrās pātiriyār, pi.ti.cīnivāca aiyaṅkār, vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar, maṟaimalai aṭikaḷ, yu.ār.ehareṉ pels, cēviyar.es taṉināyaka aṭikaḷār, ka.ta.tirunāvuk karacu, tēvanēyappāvāṇar, piriṭjeṭ maṟṟum rēmāṇṭ ālciṉ (1988), kē.kē.piḷḷai ākiyōrum aṭaṅkuvar. cuvirā jeysvāl (1974); jē.ār.mār (1975); parṭaṉ sṭeyṉ (1998) ākiyōr ‘tirāviṭar ēṟṟam’ eṉṟa koḷkaiyai ēṟkā viṭiṉum “tirāviṭar iṟakkak” eṉṟa koḷkaiyaiyum ēṟka maṟukkiṉṟaṉar.)

3. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭi intiyā vukkuḷ nuḻaintaṉar eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu mēlē pirivukaḷ (ṅa), ākiyavaṟṟil coṉṉavaṟṟōṭu porunti varukiṟatā? moḻip peruṅ kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyēyuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muntiya uṟavukaḷaip paṟṟi āyvu ceyyum aṟiñar palar ittavaṟāṉa kōṭpāṭṭiṉai oṭpam iṉṟip piṉpaṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ār.piḷeñc em.spiriks 1997il tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭa tolliyalum moḻiyum: (1) kōṭpāṭṭu āyvuneṟik karuttōṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil reṉpurū piṉvarum muṭivaik kūṟukiṟār:-

	“(mattiya kiḻakkup pakutikaḷil iruntu vēḷāṇmait toḻil ceyta makkaḷ paṇṭu paravat toṭaṅkiyatu paṟṟiya) tolliyal cāṉṟu kaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tol intō airōp piyaṉ, tol āprō - ēciyāṭik, tol elāmaiṭ, tirāviṭam, tol alṭāyk moḻi kaḷ ākiya moḻikaḷaip pēciyōr aṉaivarum mikac curuṅkiya maiyak kiḻakku nilappakutiyil (cumēriyā, elām, iṉṟaiya turukki) uṭaṉ uṟaintu irukka vēṇṭum. immoḻikaḷ ellām (nāstirāṭik moḻiyiyalāḷar kūṟuvatu pōla) toṭarpuṭaiyavaiyāka iruppatu uṇmai yāṉāl avarkaḷellām appakutiyai viṭṭup pirintu vilakiya ki.mu. 8000-6000 kālakkaṭṭattukku muṉṉar orucila āyiram āṇṭukaḷ immoḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum mūlamāṉa nāstirātik moḻi an nilappakutiyil pēcappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tolliyal kaṇṭupiṭippukkaḷukkum ottu varum tarukkamākum.”

ivvāṟu tol - nāstirātik pēciyavarkaḷ aṉai varum 10,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar oruṅkē naṭu kiḻakkup pakutiyil vāḻntiruntārkaḷ eṉṟa intak kōṭpāṭṭaiyum (ataṉ toṭarpāṉa “intiyāvukkuḷ aṅkiruntu tirāviṭarkaḷ iṟaṅkiyatu ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭittāṉ” eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭaiyum) poyppippatu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum iṭaiyē uḷḷa neruṅkiya piṇaippu ākum. itupaṟṟi kāliṉpi. mācikā 1999 il kūṟiyatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu.

“teṉ āciyāvil kaḻipaḻaṅkālattil oru potuvāṉa mūtātai moḻi iruntirukka vēṇ ṭum. taṟpōtaiya mānta iṉam (ēṟattāḻa ōr ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar) ulakeṅkum paravat toṭaṅkiya kāla kaṭṭamē tol tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālam ākalām” (It may be a question of a very ancient common substratum in South Asia, Pre-Dravidian going back even to the original peopling of the world; The year Book of South Asian Languages and Linguistics, 2001 New Delhi).

		4. ēṟattāḻa oṉṟarai ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ṉart taṟkāla mānta iṉam āppirikkāvil tōṉṟiyatu; mutal tāymoḻi ēṟattāḻa 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉatu; iṉṟaikku ōr ilaṭcam - 50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar iruntu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyākavum ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkum taṟkāla mānta iṉam paraviyatu eṉṟum mēlē kaṇṭōm. ivvāṟu vaṭakku, vaṭakiḻakku nōkkiya mānta iṉap paravalil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarukku mukāmaiyāṉa paṅku iruntirukka vēṇṭum. tirāviṭar ēṟṟam (Dravidian ascent) paṟṟiya intak kōṭpāṭṭai “ñāṉappirakācar – tēva nēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu” eṉa aḻaikkalām. (tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷukku āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neruṅkiya toṭarpaiyum (moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ piṟa vaṟṟuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṅkiya toṭarpaiyum) viḷakka vallatu ik kōṭpāṭēyākum. teṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar uruvākkiyatē cintuveḷi nākarika mākum. (hīrās 1953; mativāṇaṉ 1995; irāmanātaṉ 1999; pūraṇa cantirajīvā 2004) avarkaḷukku elām, cumēriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nākarikaṅkaḷai uruvākkiyatilum paṅku iruntirukka vēṇṭum.

5. cuvelapil (1990) “ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar tirāviṭam, urāl - alṭāyk, cappāṉiyam moḻi pēcunar kaḷiṭaiyē neruṅkiya toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu karutuvataik kaṇṭōm. attakaiya toṭarpaiyum “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” eṉṉum kōṭpāṭē viḷakkavallatu. ñāṉappirakācar - tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi ki.mu. 10,000kku muṉṉarē tol intō-airōppiyam pēcunar tol-tirāviṭam pēcu nariṭamiruntu pirintu viṭṭaṉar; maiya āciya sṭeppi pul veḷikaḷil cila āṇṭukaḷ vāḻntaṉar; avarkaḷil cila kuḻu viṉar ki.mu. 4000-3000 aḷavil mēṟku nōkki airōppāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. (avarkaḷiṭam piṉṉar uruvāṉavaiyē kirīk, ilattīṉ, kelṭik, jermāṉik, slāviya moḻik kuṭumpaṅ kaḷākum); vēṟu cila kuḻukkaḷ kiḻakku - teṉkiḻakku nōkki iṭam peyarnta kālam ki.mu. 2500ai oṭṭi ākum; avvāṟu kiḻakkē vanta kuḻuviṉar moḻikaḷil atāvatu intō āriya, irāṉiya moḻikaḷil paṇṭu (atāvatu ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar tol tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉariṭamiruntu pirinta kālat til) uṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟa tol tirāviṭa moḻik kūṟu kaḷōṭu cērttu, vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum appālum vāḻntu vanta tirāviṭa moḻipēcunarkaḷiṭam iruntu (iraṇ ṭāvatu kaṭṭamāka) putitāka mēlum pala tirāviṭa moḻik kūṟukaḷ cērkkappaṭalāyiṉa. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar ēṟṟam (Dravidian ascent)  paṟṟiya ik kōṭpāṭāṉatu tol māntar moḻiyiyal muṭivukaḷai mēlum cīrmai peṟac ceyya vallatu; nāḷaṭaivil

mānta iṉat (AMH) tōṟṟamum paravalum; moḻip peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākkamum paravalum; varalāṟṟukku muntiya tolliyal 

ākiya mūṉṟu tuṟaikaḷilum aṉaittaiyum viḷakkattakka oruṅkiṇainta oru peruṅ kōṭpāṭu (Grand synthesis) uruvākka vaḻi kōlak kūṭiyatu ākum.

6. tamatu IJDL cūṉ 2007 kaṭṭuraiyil sṭepāṉ leviṭ piṉvarumāṟu “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” koḷkaiyai ātarittuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.



“kaṭal maṭṭam ippoḻutuḷḷatai viṭa mikak kuṟaivāka irunta paṉiyūḻik kālattil āppirikkā - teṉṉintiyāvai iṇaitta vāl pōṉṟa nila iṇaippukaḷ / tīvukaḷ vaḻiyāka āp pirikkāviliruntu teṉ āciyāviṟku tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar vantirukkalāmeṉṉum kōṭ pāṭṭai pi.irāmanātaṉ valiyuṟuttukiṟār. ak kōṭpāṭṭai nāṉum ātarikkiṟēṉ. āsti 40 tamiḻar varalāṟu rēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaip paṟṟi āyvuceyta aṟiñar ammakkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumē toṭarpuḷḷavai eṉak kaṇ ṭuḷḷaṉar. uṟavumuṟai (Kinship) pūmarāṅ (vaḷaitaṭi) payaṉpāṭu ākiyavaiyum ammakkaḷukkum tirā viṭarukkum uḷḷa toṭarpaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 6000ai oṭṭi ulakeṅkum kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu nilappakutikaḷiṉ parappu curuṅkiya poḻutu ulakiṉ piṟa pakuti makkaḷuṭaiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṭam iruntu tuṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar āstirēliyappaḻaṅ kuṭi makkaḷ. avarkaḷ teṉṉintiyā viliruntu āstirēliyāvukkup pulam peyarntatu ēṟattāḻa 40,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irukkalām eṉkiṉṟa ṉar āstirēliya aṟiñar. itiliruntu kuṟaintatu 40,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tirāviṭar intiyāvil iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. inta (“tirāviṭar ēṟṟam”) kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭanāṭu celkiṉṟaṉar; piṉṉar aṅkiruntu pāracīkat tiṟkum atait tāṇṭip piṟa nilappakutikaḷukkum celkiṉṟaṉar. anta piṟa nilappakutikaḷil mūla tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpattiliruntu pirintu urā lik, altāyik, intō-airōppiyam ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. intō- airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattukku tirāviṭam tāyā? tamakkaiyā? eṉpatai iṉṟaiya nilaiyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟa iyalātu.”

iṉṟaiya marapaṇu āyvu “tol tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) - tol āstirēliya moḻi” pēcunar teṉṉintiyāviṟku iṉṟaikku 50000 āṇṭu aḷavilēyē vantuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatai mēlē 1-ṅa-10il kaṇṭōm. attuṭaṉ niṟuvuvatai oppaṉ haimar (2003)  The Real Eve : Modern Man’s Journey out of Af r i ca nūliṉpaṭi ma ra paṇu āy vu m (kākacas iṉattaic cuṭṭum ā 17) tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar intiyāviliruntu vaṭakku vaṭamēṟkāka ēṟattāḻa 40000 āṇṭukku muṉṉar maiya kiḻakku - maiya āciyā – airōppā eṉṟavāṟu paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyirukkalām eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭiṟku etirāka illai eṉpatum cuṭṭattakkatu.

		of Mankind, world civilizations, races, tribes and culture;

iyal 2

cintuveḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam

ka. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Accent) kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyil uruvākiya cintuveḷi nākarikam

1. cintuveḷi nākarikam 1947kku muntiya intiyā, pākistāṉ, vaṅkāḷa tēcam cērnta intiyāviṉ parappaḷavil kāl pakutikku mēl parappaḷavu koṇṭa peru nilap pakutiyil vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil paravi iruntatu. atāvatu cumār 5 laṭcam caturamail parappaḷavil vaḻaṅkiyatu. paṇṭaiya mecapotāmiya, ekiptiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappaiviṭa atika māṉa parappil cintuveḷi nākarikam vaḻaṅkiyatu. an nākarikam vaḻaṅkiya pakutiyil ki.mu. 2000 vākkil mottam aintiliruntu pattu laṭcam makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉpatu posel, par pōlā pōṉṟavarkaḷ karuttu ākum (appoḻutu ulakil irunta motta makkaḷ tokaiyē 9 kōṭikkum kuṟaivu). cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 1500kkum mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 20 iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē - mokeñcōtarō, harappā uṭpaṭa - akaḻvāyvukaḷ naṭan tuḷḷaṉa. cintuveḷi nākarikat toṭakkam ki.mu. 7000 liruntu eṉpatai meharkār akaḻvāyvu niṟuviyuḷḷatu. an nāka rikattiṉ ciṟappuṟṟa nilai ki.mu. 3200-1800 kāla aḷavaic cārntatu. mokañcōtarō nakara makkaḷ tokai 40,000 eṉṟum harappā nakara makkaḷ tokai 25000 eṉṟum matip piṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

2. māntar iṉa (A M H) paraval, moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Ascent)kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyē cintuveḷi nākarikap pakuti eṉṟu koḷvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷai mēlē kaṇ ṭōm. annākarikam tirāviṭac cārpuṭaiyatu eṉpataṟku uḷḷa mēlum pala valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷil cila varumāṟu:-

		i) mokañcōtārō - harappā iṭipāṭukaḷil tirāviṭak kaṭṭumāṉak kalaiyaik kāṇalām. vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nakai (āparaṇaṅ)kaḷ, parutti āṭaikaḷ aṉaittum tirāviṭac cārpuṭaiyavai.

		ii) tamiḻariṉ ciṟṟilakka muṟaiyaiyē cintuveḷi nākarika eṭai aḷavukaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉpataic ceṅkam ku. vēṅkaṭācalam niṟuviyuḷḷār.

		iii) iṉṟaiya intu matattiṉ mutaṉmaik kūṟukaḷ aṉaittum tirāviṭa - tamiḻ - cintuveḷi nākarikak kūṟukaḷē ākum. camaṇa, - putta mataṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattukkum cintuveḷi maṟṟum vaṭanāṭṭil paṇṭu irunta tamiḻ (tirāviṭa) ñāṉikaḷē kāraṇamāvar. ikkaruttai ṭailar (1973) piṉvarumāṟu kūṟuvār;

iṉṟaiya vēta matak karuttukkaḷ – avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa puttam mutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭaiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇaippu eṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉatalla. cintu veḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭaiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē, maṟu malarcciyē iṉṟaiya intumatam ākum. 
(The Hindu synthesis was less the dialectical reduction of orthodoxy and heterodoxy than the resurgence of the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)

maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum (1903,1923,1930) pi.ṭi.cīṉivāca aiyaṅkārum ikkaruttiṉarē.

iv) iṉṟaiya icaikkalai āriyar tamiḻariṭamiruntu kaṟṟatē (ō. kōsvāmi “intiya icaivaralāṟu”, 1957.)

cintuveḷi akaḻvāyvukaḷukku neṭuṅkālam muṉṉarē heviṭ (1888). maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1903) pōṉṟa nuṇ māṇuḻai pulamikka aṟiñar āriyar varukaikku muṉṉar intiyā muḻuvatum vāḻntu vanta nākarikamikka tamiḻiya makkaḷiṭamiruntē āriyar nākarikam peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttait teḷivākak kūṟiyiruntaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ virivai pi. irāmanātaṉ : cintu veḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam 1999 iyalkaḷ 3-6 maṟṟum 8 il kāṇka).

3. speyiṉ nāṭṭaic cārnta tirāviṭar eṉṟu taṉṉai aḻaittuk koṇṭa es. hīrās pātiriyār (11.9.1888 - 14. 12. 1955) toṉmai intō - naṇṇilakkarai nākarika āyvu (Studies in Proto - Indo Mediterranean Culture 1953) eṉṉum nūlil cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭaruṭaiyatu; avarkaḷ moḻi tirāviṭa moḻi eṉpatai niṟuviyuḷḷār. mikap paḻaṅ kālattil (ki.mu. 5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar) mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik karaiyil uruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiya mēṟkukkarai vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷāl cintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu naṇṇilakkarai nāṭukaḷ varai koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu eṉpatu hīrās koḷkai (avar vātat tukku ātāramākak koṇṭa ceytikaḷ cila ippoḻutu tavaṟākat tōṉṟalām; cintuveḷi muttiraikaḷai avar tirāviṭa moḻi cārntavai eṉṟu koṇṭa muṭivu cari yeṉṟālum avar muttiraikaḷil paṭittuk kaṇṭa vācakaṅkaḷ iṉṟu ēṟkattakkaṉavāka illai. iruntālum avar kaṇṭa aṭippaṭai uṇmai iṉṟum valuvuṭaiyatākavē uḷḷatu.)

4. eṉ lākōvari 1963il veḷiyiṭṭa tirāviṭar tōṟṟamum mēl nāṭukaḷum (Dravidian Origins and the West) eṉṉum nūl ciṟanta moḻiyiyal meymmaikaḷai uṇarttu vatākum. “tirāviṭar naṇṇilakkarai nāṭukaḷiliruntu pulam peyarntu vantu intiyāvil cintuveḷiyil mutaṟkaṇ kuṭiyēṟip piṉṉar kumari varai paraviṉar” eṉṟa apattakkoḷ kaiyai avar (vivaram puriyāmal) piṉpaṟṟiyirunta pōtilum avar niṟuviya piṉvarum meymmaikaḷ mukkiyamāṉavai: 

i) 6000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar intiyāviliruntu aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalvarai orē mātiriyāṉa “pala col piṇippu oṭṭunilai” (Polysynthetic Suffixal) moḻikaḷ iṭaiyīṭu iṉṟip paraviyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ ōraḷavukku oṉṟupōl iruntaṉa. potuc col viḻukkāṭu moḻikku moḻi māṟupaṭṭu iruntirukkalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, elāmaiṭ, cumēriyaṉ, harri, kākēciyaṉ, halṭi, pōṉṟavai orē moḻiyamaippuk koṇṭu oṉṟukkoṉṟu uṟavuṭaiyavaiyākac caṅkilit toṭarpōla amaintiruntaṉa.

ii) intō airōppiya moḻi pēcuvōr (ki.mu 2000ai oṭṭi) airōppāvukkuḷ kiḻakkiliruntu nuḻaiyumuṉṉar aṅku pēcappaṭṭu vanta moḻikaḷil oṉṟāṉa pāsku (yūskarā) iṉṟum pirāṉcukkum speyiṉukkum iṭaippaṭṭa pireṉīs malaippakutiyil pēcappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. tol tirāviṭa moḻi inta pāsku moḻiyuṭaṉ maṭṭumaṉṟi intō airōppiya moḻikaḷukku muṉṉar airōppāvil pēcappaṭṭa toṉ moḻikaḷuṭaṉum uṟavuṭaiyatu.

iii) immoḻikaḷ aṉaittum oṉṟukkoṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷavai; avai potuvāṉa orē tāy moḻi yiṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷ eṉṟē kūṟalām.

5. cintuveḷi nākarikam intō āriya moḻi pēcunar uruvākkiyatāka irukkalām eṉa iṉṟaiya intiya aṟiñar (kuṟippāka vaṭanāṭṭaṟiñar) cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itu tavaṟu eṉpataic caṟṟē viḷakkuvōm. vēta moḻiyākiya intō airōppiya moḻi intiyāvil uruvākiyirukkalām eṉṟu vēta, camaskirutappaṟṟāḷar kūṟuvatu apattam [ituvēṟu; ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar toltirāviṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷaitta moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷuḷ intō airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpamum oṉṟu eṉṟa ñāṉappirakācar – tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu vēṟu!] ki.mu. 1700 - 1500 kālakaṭṭattil intiyā viṟkuḷ vaṭamēṟkiliruntu nuḻaintavarkaḷ vētamoḻi (Vedic Language)ākiya intō āriya moḻi pēcunar āvar. ivar kaḷ cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷōṭu oppiṭum pōtu ciṟu eṇṇikkaiyiṉarākavē iruntirukka vēṇṭum. intō airōppiya moḻip peruṅkuṭumpattil aṭaṅkiyavai intō āriya moḻiyākiya vētamoḻi tavira irāṉiyaṉ (avestaṉ), aṉaṭōliyaṉ (hiṭṭaiṭ), armīṉiyaṉ, ṭōkkāriyaṉ, alpēṉiyaṉ, kirīkku, ittālik (ilattīṉ mutaliyavai), kelṭik (airiṣ, velṣ uṭpaṭa), jermāṉik (āṅkilam uṭpaṭa), pālṭik (laṭviyaṉ, littuvēṉiyaṉ), slāv (raṣyaṉmutaliyavai) ākiyavaiyākum. intap pal vēṟu intō airōppiya moḻi pēcunarum oruṅku cērntu ki.mu. 4000 - 3000 kāla aḷavil karuṅkaṭal - kāspiyaṉ kaṭal pakutikku vaṭakkil mattiya āciya - airōppiya sṭeppi pulveḷikaḷil vacittu vanta nāṭōṭikaḷ (nomads)āvar. ak kālakkaṭṭattil avarkaḷ intō airōppiyat toṉ moḻiyaip pēcivantaṉar. ki. mu. 3000 - 2000 kāla aḷavil teṉ kiḻakkākavum nakaralāyiṉar. mēṟkil ceṉṟa nāṭōṭik kuḻuviṉaruḷ oṉṟāṉa kirīkkumoḻi pēcunar nuḻainta pakuti yil (tirāviṭac cārpāṉa) appakutip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu kirīkku moḻi pēcunar uṭaṉaṭiyāka nākarikam peṟṟaṉar. kiḻakku teṉkiḻakkākap peyarnta nāṭōṭik kuḻuviṉaruḷ intō āriya moḻi pēcum kuḻuvum oṉṟu. ki.mu. 1700 - 1500il avarkaḷ intiyāvukkuḷ irāṉiliruntu nuḻainta poḻutu avarkaḷum cintuveḷit tol nākarikattiṉariṭa miruntu viraivil nākarikam peṟṟaṉar. cintuveḷit tirāviṭa aṟiñarkaḷ (avarkaḷil cilar riṣikaḷākavum ēṟkappaṭṭaṉar) paṭaippukaḷum nēraṭiyākavō moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭō rik vētattilēyē ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ (1903, 1923, 1930), vālparṭ pōṉṟōr karuttākum.

6. taṅkaḷuṭaiya The rise of civilisation in India and Pakistan  (1988)nūlil piriṭjaṭ maṟṟum rēmāṇṭ ālciṉ tampatiyar. 
karuttum ituvē :

	(The earliest Indo-Aryan text, the compiled Rg Veda, shows several influences of a non-Indo-Aryan, Dravidian element, in the form of phonetic changes, introduction of loan words and names etc.These presuppose the coexistence of the Vedic and Dravidian speaking peoples in a cultural contact situation for a period, perhaps of centuries, before the compilation of the Rg Veda (circa 1500- 1300BC)

mutaṟkaṇ intō āriya moḻi pēcunarukkum cintu veḷi nākarikat tamiḻarukkum (tirāviṭarukkum) iṭaiyē vaṉ muṟai ēṟpaṭṭa pōtilum viraivil iruvakaiyārum kalantu viṭṭaṉar. āriyarāl nakaraṅkaḷ tākkappaṭṭirukkalām. intira ṉiṉ peyarē purantaraṉ (nakaraṅkaḷai aḻippavaṉ) tāṉē! nakaraṅkaḷiṉ vīḻccikku ki.mu. 1700ai oṭṭi nikaḻnta iyaṟkai, cuṟṟup puṟac cūḻnilaik kāraṇaṅkaḷum ōraḷavu kāraṇamāyirun tirukkalām eṉṟu ālciṉ (1995) kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl cintu veḷi nākarikam paraviyirunta ciṟṟūrppuṟap perunilap parappil (ciṟu eṇṇikkaiyil nuḻainta) āriyarāl peruntāk kam ēṟpaṭuttiyirukka muṭiyātu. ākavētāṉ cunītikumār cāṭṭarji, vālṭar pērcarvīs, es.ē.ṭailar pōṉṟōr kūṟuvatupōla iṉṟaiya nākarikam, paṇpāṭu, intumatam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭittaḷam (rūpāykku 12 aṇā aḷavukku eṉpār caṭṭarji) tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi, paṇpāṭu āki yavaiyē. intō āriya moḻi pēcunar paraval paṟṟiya varalāṟṟiliruntē avarkaḷ ki.mu. 1700kku muntiya cintu nākarikattai uruvākkiyirukka iyalātu eṉpatu teriyum. cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil āriyarukku nerukkamāṉa kutirai kiṭaiyātu eṉpatum mukkiyamāṉatu.

7. intō āriya moḻiyiṉ rikvēta nilaiyilēyē nuḻaintuḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷāka cumār nāṟpatu – aimpatu coṟkaḷai parō, emaṉō pōṉṟavarkaḷ ēṟkeṉavē aṭai yāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal - ōr aṟi mukam (1990) nūlil itaik kuṟippiṭum cuvelapil, rik vētak kālattiliruntē intō āriya moḻi kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷa ēṟattāḻa 10 moḻiyiyaṟ kūṟukaḷaiyum paṭṭiyaliṭṭuḷḷār. [ivaiyellām vēta camaṟkiruta moḻiyil tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ tākkam eṉṟa aḷaviltāṉ ivarkaḷ āyvukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl ñāṉap pirakācarum pāvāṇarum kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷ ivaṟṟaiviṭa mika viñciyavai. avviruvarum “intō - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai vērc coṟkaḷ palavum tol - tirāviṭa moḻiyiliruntu peṟṟavaiyē” eṉṟa karuttuṭaiyavarkaḷ.]

8. cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟum an nākarika muttirai eḻuttukkaḷ tol tamiḻē eṉṟum takuti vāynta aṟiñar aṉaivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. Indus Script Dravidian (1995) nūlil irā. mativāṇaṉ, cintu veḷiyil muntu tamiḻ (2004) nūlil pūrṇa cantira jīvā ākiyōr av veḻuttukkaḷait toltamiḻākap paṭikkum poḻutu tol kāppiyattiliruntu kiṭṭum moḻiyiyal taravukaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. puṟanāṉūṟu 201, 202il kuṟippiṭappaṭum iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr cintuveḷi nākarikattōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyavarkaḷākak kapilarāl karutappaṭṭaṉar. puṟanāṉūṟu 202 kuṟippiṭum araiyam rikvētam 6.27. 5il cuṭṭappaṭum hariyūpiyā tāṉ eṉpatu pi.el. cāmi karuttu (centamiḻc celvi caṉavari 1994). araiyam = aracamaram. araiya + akappā = araiyakappā = harappā eṉṟu peyar māṟiyatu eṉkiṟār avar. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr 49 talaimuṟaikaḷukku muṉṉar (atāvatu tol paḻaṅkālattil) pukaḻpeṟṟa tuvarai eṉṉum kōṭṭai nakarai āṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu puṟam 201 kūṟukiṟatu. tamatu tolkāppiyap pāyira vuraiyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vēḷir tuvārapati (tuvārakai)yiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉkiṉṟār. araiyam harappāvaittāṉ kuṟippatākak koṇṭāl harappā paṟṟiyum tuvārakai paṟṟiyum kapilar kālattil vaḻaṅkiya (oru nakarukkuriyatai maṟṟataṟkuriyatāka māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiya) toṉmak karuttaip puṟam 201, 202 pāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉka. itaṉai jīvā mēlum āyvu ceykiṟār. vēḷ-vēṭ-pēṭ (Bet) - pēṭ tuvārakā eṉṟu tuvārakaiyiṉ peyar varalāṟṟai avar tarukiṟār.

9. cumēriya nākarika muttiraikaḷil ciṅkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭaik kollum kilkāmeṣ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kilkāmeṣ paṟṟiya toṉmak kataiyum cumēriyap poṟippukaḷil tarap paṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷi muttiraikaḷ iraṇṭil (kilkāmēṣ ciṅkaṅkaḷait tākkuvatu pōlavē) intiya vīraṉ oruvaṉ pulikaḷ iraṇṭai kaikku oṉṟākak kolvatu pōl cittirikkappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. puṟam 201 iruṅkōvēḷaip ‘puli
kaṭimāl’ eṉṟu aḻaikkiṟatu. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr kālam cintuveḷi nākarikak kālattiliruntu toṭaṅki yirukkalām eṉa ai. makātēvaṉ Journal of Tamil Studies mē
1970 itaḻil uṉṉittiruntār. āyiṉum 2002 caṉavariyil avar karuttu anta uṉṉippiṟku valuvāṉa ātāram illai eṉpatākum.

10. rikvētam ai. 133, 1 & 3iṉ āṅkila vaṭivam varumāṟu;

		“ō makavāṉ! aḻintupaṭṭa vailastāṉaka nakarattilum,
		aḻintupaṭṭa makāvailasta nakarattilum uḷḷa cūṉiyak
		kārikaḷ kumpalkaḷai aḻittu ōḻippāyāka”

		“nāṉ mēlulakattaiyum pūmiyaiyum cattiyattiṉāl
		tūymaippaṭuttukiṟēṉ. vailastāṉa nakaril intiraṉāl
		tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭu kolaiyuṇṭu kiṭakkum (intiraṉai
		eritta) āṟṟal mikka tuṣṭap picācukaḷai nāṉ erittu
		oḻikkiṟēṉ”

āriyarkaḷāl nācamākkappaṭṭa nakarattiṉ peyarāṉa ‘vailas tāṉam’ eṉpatu āriyamoḻiyallāta piṟamoḻic collāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu ṭākṭar parō karuttu ākum. (jarṉal āp intiyaṉ hisṭari XII - 1 ēpral 1963,) orukkāl ‘vailastāṉam’ eṉpatu vēḷiruṭaiya ūr/nakaraik kuṟittirukkalām.

11. cintu mutaliya āṟukaḷil cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṭṭiyirunta aṇaikaḷai āriyar uṭaittu nāṭṭai veḷḷak kāṭākki aḻittirukkalām eṉpar tāmōtar tarmāṉant kōcampi (The culture and Civilisation of Ancient India in historical
outline 1974). maturaik kāñci 725ām aṭiyilum aka nāṉūṟu 346ām pāṭalilum āṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē kaṭṭappaṭum aṇaikaḷaik kuṟikka ‘kaṟciṟai’ eṉṉum col payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. rik vētattil aṇaiyaik kuṟippiṭum cirā (Sira) eṉṉum col ‘kaṟciṟaiyiṉ’ citaivē eṉpar pi.el. cāmi (centamiḻc celvi : 1994 navampar).


akaḻvāyvil kiṭaittuḷḷa mukkiyamāṉa
cintuveḷi nākarikat talaṅkaḷ



u. cintuveḷi nākarika muttirai eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻiya
moḻiyākap paṭikkattakkavaiyē eṉpatum tamiḻ vari
vaṭivam cintuveḷi varivaṭivattiliruntē
uruvākiyirukkalām eṉpatum

1. cintuveḷi nākarika muttirai eḻuttukkaḷ eṉpavai mūvāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa “sṭīyaṭṭaiṭ” (cavarkkārakkal, atāvatu mākkal) muttiraikaḷilum cila cepput takaṭukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟilum uḷḷavai. ovvoru muttiraiyum cumār 20 mi.mī. x 30 mi.mī. aḷavuḷḷatu. cila caturamāṉavai. perum pālaṉavaṟṟil oru vilaṅkiṉ uruvamum ataṉ mēl pakkattil oṉṟiliruntu pattup paṉṉiraṇṭu (carācari 5) kuṟiyīṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. mika nīṇṭa toṭar 26 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ koṇṭatu. cintu veḷiyiliruntu paruttit tuṇi pōṉṟavaṟṟai cumēriyā pōṉṟa mēlaināṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyumpoḻutu
cippaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi avaṟṟiṉmēl poruḷukkuriyavar peyaraik kaḷimaṇṇil muttiraiyiṭa im muttiraikaḷuḷ perum pālāṉavai payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatē ciṟanta āyvaṟiñar kaḷ karuttu. vīlar, haṇṭar, kāṭ (Gadd), kōcampi, kō (Coe) ākiyōr inta aṭippaṭaiyiltāṉ muttiraik kuṟiyīṭukaḷil uḷḷavai taṉi āṭkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷākat tāṉ (cila nērvu kaḷil paṭṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ) irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivukku vantuḷḷaṉar. cippattaik kaṭṭiya kayiṟu, pāy ivaṟṟiṉ cuvaṭukaḷ cila kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (cila kaṭṭikaḷ orō vaḻi neruppiṉ vāyppāṭṭu cuṭappaṭṭatāl avai maṭṭumē iṉṟu kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiltāṉ iv vaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa) āka, ālciṉ (1988) kūṟuvatu pōl “im muttiraikaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭu (allatu payaṉpāṭu kaḷil oṉṟu) vāṇika naṭavaṭikkaikaḷ cārntatāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamillai” (There can be little doubt that
the Harappan seals were - atleast as one of their functions – necessary elements in the mechanism of trade. p. 185.)cila muttiraikaḷ tāyat tukkaḷākavum nērttik kaṭaṉ villaikaḷākavum aṭaiyāḷa ilacciṉaikaḷākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu irukkalām.

2. pala muttiraikaḷil oṟṟaik kompu teriyum erutu oṉṟiṉ muṉṉar eṉṟa vaṭivam uḷḷatu. makātēvaṉ karuttu (1985, 1984) ivvaṭivam cōmaccāṟu vaṭikkappaṭṭa ēṉam; piṉṉar cōmaccāṟu āriyarkaḷālum mukkiyamāṉatāka ēṟkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. piṟkālattil ivvaṭivam intirat vajam āṉatu. aḷakkuṭi cīttārāmaṉ karūril kaṇṭeṭutta ceppu muttiraiyilum inta vaṭivam uḷḷatu eṉpatu makā tēvaṉ karuttu ākum.

3. cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ēṟattāḻa 400 aḷavil uḷḷaṉa. (parpōlā 385, makātēvaṉ 417). 2906 muttirai vācakaṅkaḷil kaṇṭa kuṟiyīṭukaḷai makātēvaṉ piṉvarumāṟu vakaippaṭuttukiṟār:
